# AlianHub API reference

Generated by `npm run api:doc` from the routes the server registers, the guards they sit behind and `scripts/api-doc.meta.json`. Do not edit this file by hand. It is regenerated in the docs pull request that follows merges to `beta`, so in between it can be a few routes behind the code: `npm run api:doc:check` says whether it is current, and a route added since is not here yet.

Built from 949 routes and the meta file, fingerprint `25a2cd1068fc`.

The server registers 949 routes. 65 are documented in full below. The other 884 are listed in the [appendix](#appendix-every-other-route) with their method, path and auth, marked `internal` or `undocumented`, so this page is honest about what it does not cover. A machine-readable list of paths, methods, auth and tags is in `docs/api/openapi.json`.

Every id, name and token below is a placeholder.

## Base URL and versions

Every path is relative to the address your AlianHub instance is served from, for example `https://hub.example.com`. There is no separate API host.

| Prefix | What it is |
|---|---|
| `/api/v2` | The API integrations should use. This reference documents the part of it a token can call. |
| `/api/public-v1` | A small read-only namespace that accepts an API token and nothing else. |
| `/api/v1` | The web app's own API. It is listed in the appendix so nothing is hidden, but it follows the web app and may change without notice. |

Request bodies are JSON (`Content-Type: application/json`). A body larger than the server's limit (2 MB unless the instance raises `BODY_LIMIT`) is refused.

## Authentication

### Personal access tokens

A personal access token starts with `ahp_` and is shown once, when it is created. Send it as a bearer token, together with the id of the workspace the request is for:

```http
GET /api/v2/api-tokens/me
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

A token acts as the person who created it. It reads what that person can open in the web app and changes what that person's role allows; it is never a way past project or sprint visibility. A token stops working on its next call when it is revoked, when it expires, or when its person is removed from the workspace.

A token is created from a signed-in session, in the web app. A token cannot create, change or delete tokens: the token management routes answer 403 to a token, except `GET /api/v2/api-tokens/me`.

### The `companyid` header

A token belongs to one workspace. Every request made with a token carries that workspace's id in the `companyid` header, as 24 hexadecimal characters. A missing or malformed header is a 401.

### Scopes

A token carries the scopes `read` and `write`. `GET`, `HEAD` and `OPTIONS` requests need `read`; every other method needs `write`, including a `POST` that only reads. A token without the scope a request needs gets a 403. A token created with no scopes named holds both.

### Tokens limited to projects

A token can be created limited to some projects. Such a token is refused, with 403 and `code: "token_limited_to_projects"`, on every route that cannot hold it to that list. Of the routes documented here, these accept it:

- `GET /api/v2/api-tokens/me`
- `GET /api/public-v1/projects`
- `GET /api/public-v1/tasks`
- `GET /api/public-v1/tasks/:key`
- `POST /api/v2/tasks/everything`

On those routes it reads only its own projects; a project outside the list answers "not found". Each route below says whether it accepts such a token.

### Agent tokens

A token created for an AI agent is an agent token. On top of everything above, an agent token is refused on every `DELETE` request, on the bulk task route, and on billing, member, permission and token management routes. Those refusals are 403 and are recorded in the audit log.

### Token lifetime

A token can be given an expiry of 1 to 365 days when it is created. An instance running with `API_TOKEN_STRICT` requires both an expiry and at least one scope, can lower the longest lifetime with `API_TOKEN_MAX_DAYS`, and stops tokens that have no expiry 30 days after strict mode began (or after the token was created, if later). Without strict mode a token with no expiry keeps working until it is revoked.

### OAuth

OAuth is offered for the MCP endpoint (`/mcp`) when the instance turns `MCP_OAUTH` on, and only there: an OAuth access token is not accepted by the REST routes in this reference. Its scopes are `tasks:read`, `tasks:write`, `projects:read`, `docs:read`, `time:read`, `time:write`, `tasks:manage`, `docs:manage`, `chat:read`. By default an access token lasts 15 minutes, a refresh token 30 days and a grant at most 90 days. See `docs/MCP-AGENT-GUIDE.md`.

## Responses and errors

A success is a JSON object with `status: true`, a human-readable `statusText`, and the result in `data`:

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "Pages fetched.", "data": [] }
```

A few routes put a value beside `data` instead of inside it (a new task's `id`, a list's `hasMore`); each route below shows its own response.

A failure is a JSON object with `status: false` and the reason in `statusText`, `message` or both:

```json
{ "status": false, "statusText": "Page not found." }
```

Some failures add `error`, a machine-readable `code`, the `permission` key that was missing, or the `field` that was refused.

**Status codes.** The web app expects HTTP 200 on a handled failure and reads `status` from the body. A request made with an API token gets a real status code instead: when a handler answers 200 with `status: false`, the server changes the status line to the matching 4xx and adds the header `X-Status-Mapped: 1`. The body is not changed. Any other caller can ask for the same with the header `Prefer: status-codes`. The code is the handler's own `statusCode` when it sets one, and is otherwise read from the text: "not found" is 404, a permission refusal is 403, "already" or "duplicate" is 409, a token or session problem is 401, and anything else is 400. Check `status` in the body as well as the status code.

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| 400 | The request was refused: a missing or malformed field, an unknown key, a value out of range. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the scope, the person's role lacks the permission, or the token may not use the route. |
| 404 | The thing does not exist, or the caller may not see it. The two are answered alike on purpose. |
| 409 | The request conflicts with what is stored. |
| 429 | Rate limited. See [Rate limiting](#rate-limiting). |
| 500 | The server failed. The body carries `status: false`. |

## Rate limiting

There is one limit per client address, across all API routes: 1000 requests per 60 seconds unless the instance sets `GLOBAL_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MIN` (which can also turn it off). Static files are not counted. A request over the limit is answered with 429, a `Retry-After` header in seconds, and:

```json
{
  "status": false,
  "code": "server_busy",
  "statusText": "The server is busy. Try again in a moment.",
  "message": "The server is busy. Try again in a moment.",
  "retryAfter": 12
}
```

On a 429, wait `retryAfter` seconds and send the same request again. The response headers `RateLimit-Limit`, `RateLimit-Remaining` and `RateLimit-Reset` show where the caller stands.

## Ids and dates

An id is 24 hexadecimal characters, sent and returned as a string. A value that is not a well-formed id where one is expected is a 400, or reads as "not found".

A date is returned as an ISO 8601 string in UTC, for example `2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z`. Where a route accepts a date it accepts an ISO 8601 string; a route that also accepts a time in milliseconds says so.

Field names are the stored ones and are not uniform: a task carries `TaskName`, `ProjectID` and `Task_Priority` beside `sprintId` and `statusKey`. Use the names exactly as each route shows them.

## Pagination

Three styles are in use. Each route says which one it follows.

| Style | How it works | Used by |
|---|---|---|
| Cursor | Send the `nextCursor` of the page before as `cursor`. `nextCursor` is `null` on the last page. | `POST /api/v2/tasks/everything` |
| Skip and limit | Send `skip` and `limit`. The response carries `hasMore` and `nextSkip`. | `GET /api/v2/webhooks/:id/logs` |
| Limit only | Send `limit`, up to the route's cap. The newest rows come first and there is no next page. | `GET /api/public-v1/tasks`, `GET /api/v2/agents/runs`, `GET /api/v2/agents/proposals` |

Routes that are not in this table return the whole list.

## Webhook deliveries

A webhook posts task events to a URL you choose. The events are `task.created`, `task.updated`, `task.deleted`, `task.archived`, `task.restored`; `*` subscribes to all of them. The formats are `json`, `slack`, `discord`. Webhooks are managed with the routes under [Webhooks](#webhooks).

A delivery in the `json` format is a `POST` with this body:

```json
{
  "event": "task.updated",
  "companyId": "<company id>",
  "deliveredAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
  "changedFields": ["Task_Priority"],
  "data": {
    "_id": "<task id>",
    "TaskKey": "WEB-12",
    "TaskName": "Write the release notes",
    "statusType": "active",
    "Task_Priority": "HIGH",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "sprintId": "<list id>",
    "AssigneeUserId": ["<user id>"],
    "assigneeNames": ["Sample Person"],
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is a fixed set of task fields, never the whole stored task. `previous` is added, with the same shape, when the server has delivered this task before. Each delivery carries the headers `X-AlianHub-Event`, `X-AlianHub-Delivery-Attempt` and `X-AlianHub-Signature`. The signature is `sha256=` followed by the HMAC-SHA256 of the raw request body, keyed with the secret returned when the webhook was created; compute it over the bytes received and compare before trusting a delivery. Changes to one task within a couple of seconds arrive as one delivery. A delivery that fails with a network error or a 5xx is sent once more, 30 seconds later.

## Stability

| Mark | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `stable` | Meant to be relied on. The request and response shapes shown here are not expected to change. |
| `beta` | Usable, but the shape may still change with the feature it serves. |
| `internal` | For the web app, instance administration or sign-in. Not for integrations; it may change at any time. |
| `undocumented` | Not described in full yet. It appears in the appendix only. |

## Identity

Who a token is, and what that person may do.

### Who am I

`GET /api/v2/api-tokens/me` · stable

Returns the person and the workspace behind the token, with that person's role and permissions.

Call it once after connecting, to check that the token and the `companyid` header are right.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Accepted, and held to its projects |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/api-tokens/me
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Identity resolved.",
  "data": {
    "userId": "<user id>",
    "companyId": "<company id>",
    "name": "Sample Person",
    "email": "person@example.com",
    "timeZone": "Europe/London",
    "roleType": 3,
    "permissions": { "task.task_list": true, "task.task_create": true, "project.project_create": null },
    "scopes": ["read", "write"],
    "tokenName": "Reporting script"
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `roleType` | `1` is the owner, `2` an admin, `3` a member, `0` a guest. |
| `permissions` | The person's workspace-wide answer for the permission keys the server checks most: `true` may change, `false` may only read, `null` has no access, and `1` or `2` mean "own" or "everyone" on the keys that take a choice. A project with its own rules can answer differently. |
| `scopes` | The token's scopes. Empty when the request was made with a session. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Read-only token API

Three reads under `/api/public-v1` that accept an API token and nothing else. They are the simplest way to list projects and tasks. Every read is narrowed to what the token's person can open in the web app, and a token limited to projects reads only those.

### List projects

`GET /api/public-v1/projects` · stable

Lists the projects the token's person can open.

Called with an API token that holds the `read` scope, for a person who is still a member of the workspace. Answers with `data`: up to 200 projects that are not deleted and that the token's person can open, each with `ProjectName`, `ProjectCode` and `createdAt`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Accepted, and held to its projects |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/public-v1/projects
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<project id>",
      "ProjectName": "Website",
      "ProjectCode": "WEB",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

There is no `statusText` on a success here. Projects in the trash are left out.

**Paging**

None. At most 200 projects are returned.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List a project's tasks

`GET /api/public-v1/tasks` · stable

Lists the tasks of one project, most recently updated first.

Called with an API token that holds the `read` scope. It reads the query field `projectId` (a 24-character id, required) and `limit` (a number, clamped to 1 to 500, 100 when left out); a missing or malformed `projectId` answers 400, and a project the token's person cannot open answers 404. Answers with `data`: the project's tasks that are not deleted, most recently updated first, each with `TaskKey`, `TaskName`, `status`, `statusType`, `Task_Priority`, `AssigneeUserId`, `DueDate`, `sprintId`, `epicId`, `createdAt` and `updatedAt`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Accepted, and held to its projects |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id | yes | The project. |
| `limit` | number |  | How many tasks to return, from 1 to 500. 100 when left out. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/public-v1/tasks?projectId=<project id>&limit=50
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<task id>",
      "TaskKey": "WEB-12",
      "TaskName": "Write the release notes",
      "status": { "key": 2, "text": "Doing", "type": "active" },
      "statusType": "active",
      "Task_Priority": "HIGH",
      "AssigneeUserId": ["<user id>"],
      "DueDate": "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z",
      "sprintId": "<list id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

Each task carries these fields and `epicId` when it has one, and no others. Deleted tasks are left out, and so are tasks in lists the person cannot see.

**Paging**

Limit only. There is no next page: narrow by project and raise `limit`, or use `POST /api/v2/tasks/everything` to page.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `projectId` is missing or is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. |
| 404 | The project does not exist, or the person cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a task by its key

`GET /api/public-v1/tasks/:key` · stable

Returns one task, looked up by its key.

Called with an API token that holds the `read` scope. The path value is the task key as a string of at most 40 characters; an empty or longer one answers 400, and a task the token's person cannot open or that does not exist answers 404. Answers with `data`: the stored task.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Accepted, and held to its projects |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `key` | string | The task's key, for example `WEB-12`. At most 40 characters. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/public-v1/tasks/WEB-12
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "data": {
    "_id": "<task id>",
    "TaskKey": "WEB-12",
    "TaskName": "Write the release notes",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "sprintId": "<list id>",
    "status": { "key": 2, "text": "Doing", "type": "active" },
    "statusKey": 2,
    "statusType": "active",
    "Task_Priority": "HIGH",
    "AssigneeUserId": ["<user id>"],
    "DueDate": "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the whole stored task; the example shows some of its fields.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The key is longer than 40 characters. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. |
| 404 | No task has that key, or the person cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Tasks

Creating a task and changing one. Both routes take the bodies the web app sends, so their field names follow the stored task. To read tasks, use [Everything](#everything) or the [Read-only token API](#read-only-token-api).

### Create a task

`POST /api/v2/tasks` · beta

Creates a task, or a subtask when `data.ParentTaskId` names a parent.

The body is the one the web app sends: the task under `data` and the project it goes into under `projectData`. The server sets the company and the acting person itself, whatever the body says. Under a parent, the project, list and folder are taken from the parent, and the parent must be in the project the body names.

The task's key is assigned after the task is saved, from the project's code and its next number. Read the task back to learn it.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | `task.task_create` |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `data.TaskName` | string | yes | The task's name. |
| `data.TaskKey` | string | yes | A placeholder such as `-`. The server replaces it with the real key. |
| `data.TaskType` | string | yes | One of the project's task types, by its value. |
| `data.TaskTypeKey` | number |  | The key of that task type. |
| `data.ProjectID` | id | yes | The project the task goes into. |
| `data.sprintId` | id |  | The list the task goes into. |
| `data.sprintArray` | object |  | `{ id, name }` of that list, with `folderId` and `folderName` when the list sits in a folder. |
| `data.folderObjId` | id |  | The folder, when the list sits in one. |
| `data.status` | object |  | The status as the project defines it: `{ text, key, type }`. |
| `data.statusKey` | number |  | The key of that status. |
| `data.statusType` | string |  | The type of that status, for example `default_active`. |
| `data.Task_Priority` | string |  | The priority, for example `MEDIUM`. |
| `data.AssigneeUserId` | id[] |  | The assignees. Each must hold a seat in the workspace. |
| `data.watchers` | id[] |  | The watchers. Each must hold a seat in the workspace. |
| `data.Task_Leader` | id |  | The task's lead. Must hold a seat in the workspace. |
| `data.DueDate` | date |  | The due date, or an empty string. With a date, send `data.dueDateDeadLine` as `[{ date }]`. |
| `data.startDate` | date |  | The start date. |
| `data.ParentTaskId` | id |  | The parent, for a subtask. Empty for a top-level task. |
| `data.isParentTask` | boolean |  | `true` for a top-level task, `false` for a subtask. |
| `data.deletedStatusKey` | number |  | `0` for a live task. |
| `projectData._id` | id |  | The same project as `data.ProjectID`. |
| `projectData.ProjectName` | string |  | The project's name, used in the notification. |
| `projectData.ProjectCode` | string |  | The project's code. The task's key starts with it. |
| `indexObj` | object |  | Where the task sits in the web app's grouped lists: `{ indexName, searchKey, searchValue }`. `indexName` is one of `groupByStatusIndex`, `groupByPriorityIndex`, `groupByAssigneeIndex`, `groupByDueDateIndex`. |

`data` may carry any other field a stored task has. A field a task does not have is dropped. `_id`, `createdBy` and `createdAt` are refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/tasks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "data": {
    "TaskName": "Write the release notes",
    "TaskKey": "-",
    "TaskType": "task",
    "TaskTypeKey": 1,
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "sprintId": "<list id>",
    "sprintArray": { "id": "<list id>", "name": "Sprint 12" },
    "status": { "text": "To Do", "key": 1, "type": "default_active" },
    "statusKey": 1,
    "statusType": "default_active",
    "Task_Priority": "MEDIUM",
    "AssigneeUserId": [],
    "watchers": [],
    "DueDate": "",
    "dueDateDeadLine": [],
    "ParentTaskId": "",
    "isParentTask": true,
    "deletedStatusKey": 0
  },
  "projectData": { "_id": "<project id>", "ProjectName": "Website", "ProjectCode": "WEB" },
  "indexObj": { "indexName": "groupByStatusIndex", "searchKey": "statusKey", "searchValue": 1 }
}
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "Task created successfully.", "id": "<task id>" }
```

The new task's id is in `id`, beside `status`, not inside `data`.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is not an object, carries a key that is not accepted, names something that is not an id where an id is expected, names a person who holds no seat in the workspace, the parent cannot take another level of subtasks, or the workspace's plan allows no more tasks in the list. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 404 | The parent task does not exist, or is not in the project the body names. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a task

`PATCH /api/v2/tasks` · beta

Changes one task. `action` names the change, and the other keys of the body depend on it.

Each action reads the task it changes from the id shown under Ids, and answers 404 when the caller cannot see that task. The first id listed is the task; the others are the further tasks, people or projects the action names.

The server fills in the acting person, the company and, where an action uses them, the stored names of the task and its project. Keys an action does not use are dropped rather than refused, so the bodies the web app sends keep working. Where a key is followed by names in brackets, those are the only fields of that object the action writes.

Some actions take `isUpdateTask`. Leave it out. Sent as `false` it records history for a change another request already made, and is refused with 409 unless the stored task already holds that value.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | One per action, in the table below |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | string | yes | One of the actions in the table below. |

**Actions**

| `action` | Permission | Ids | Body keys kept | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `updateStatus` | `task.task_status` | `task._id`, `prevStatus.taskId` | `newStatus` (`status`, `statusKey`, `statusType`), `prevStatus`, `projectData`, `task`, `isUpdateTask` | Sets the status. `newStatus` holds the status as the project defines it. |
| `updatePriority` | `task.task_priority` | `taskData._id`, `priorityObj.taskId` | `firebaseObj` (`Task_Priority`, `Updated_At`), `projectData`, `taskData`, `priorityObj`, `isUpdateTask` | Sets the priority. |
| `updateAssignee` | `task.task_assignee` | `taskData._id` | `firebaseObj` (`AssigneeUserId`), `projectData`, `taskData`, `employeeName`, `type`, `isUpdateTask` | `type` is `assigneeAdd`, `assigneRemove` or `replace`. |
| `updateDueDate` | `task.task_due_date` | `task._id` | `commonDateFormatString`, `timeZone`, `firebaseObj` (`DueDate`, `dueDateDeadLine`), `project`, `task`, `obj`, `isUpdateTask` |  |
| `updateStartDate` | `task.task_due_date` | `task._id` | `commonDateFormatString`, `timeZone`, `firebaseObj` (`startDate`), `project`, `task`, `obj`, `isUpdateTask`, `isHistory` |  |
| `updateTaskType` | `task.task_type` | `taskData._id` | `newStatus` (`TaskType`, `TaskTypeKey`, `taskTypeImage`, `oldTaskTypeImage`, `taskTypeName`), `prevStatus`, `projectData`, `taskData`, `isUpdateTask` |  |
| `updateDescription` | `task.task_description` | `task._id` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `sprintId`, `task`, `text` | Sets the description from `text.blocks` and `text.text`. |
| `updateTaskTotalEstimate` | `task.task_estimated_hours` | `taskData._id` | `firebaseObj` (`totalEstimatedTime`), `projectData`, `taskData`, `obj` |  |
| `updatePoints` | `task.task_estimated_hours` | `taskData._id` | `firebaseObj` (`points`), `projectData`, `taskData` |  |
| `createSubTaskWithAi` | Depends on the body | `parentTask.id`, `parentTask.ProjectID` | `companyId`, `userId`, `subTitles`, `sprintObj`, `projectData`, `parentTask`, `type` | Needs `task.sub_task_create` when `type` is `subTask`, otherwise `task.task_create`. |
| `updateTaskName` | `task.task_name_edit` | `taskData._id` | `firebaseObj` (`TaskName`), `projectData`, `taskData`, `obj` | Renames the task. |
| `updateDates` | `task.task_due_date` | `taskData._id` | `firebaseObj` (`startDate`, `DueDate`), `projectData`, `taskData` |  |
| `updateStartDateAndDueDate` | `task.task_due_date`, `task.task_start_date` | `task._id` | `commonDateFormatString`, `timeZone`, `notificationObj`, `firebaseObj` (`DueDate`, `dueDateDeadLine`, `startDate`), `task`, `project` |  |
| `updateTaskLeader` | `task.task_assignee` | `taskData._id`, `firebaseObj.Task_Leader` | `firebaseObj` (`Task_Leader`), `projectData`, `taskData`, `employeeName`, `isUpdateTask` |  |
| `updateWatcher` | `task.task_list` | `taskId`, `userId` | `companyId`, `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `userId`, `add`, `employeeName` |  |
| `updateTags` | `task.task_tag` | `taskId` | `companyId`, `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `tagId`, `operation` |  |
| `updateChecklists` | Depends on the body | `taskId` | `companyId`, `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `operation`, `data`, `historyObj`, `taskData` | Assigning or unassigning an item needs `task.task_checklist_assign_remove`. Every other operation needs `task.task_checklist`. |
| `AddAiChecklist` | `task.task_checklist` | `taskId` | `companyId`, `taskId`, `checklistArray`, `sprintId`, `projectId` |  |
| `updateAttachments` | `task.task_attachments` | `taskId`, `taskData._id` | `companyId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `taskData`, `id`, `operation`, `data`, `projectData` |  |
| `updateTaskCustomField` | `task.task_custom_field` | `taskId` | `companyId`, `taskId`, `updateDetail`, `customFieldId` | Sets one custom field's value: `customFieldId`, and `updateDetail` with the field's id as `_id` and the value as `fieldValue`. A value that does not fit the field's type is refused. |
| `updateMarkAsFavourite` | `task.task_list` | `taskId`, `updateDetail` | `companyId`, `taskId`, `updateDetail`, `type` |  |
| `updateLastMessageTime` | `chat.one_to_one_chat` | `taskId` | `companyId`, `taskId`, `msgObj` |  |
| `updateQueueList` | `task.queue_list` | `taskId`, `userId` | `CompanyId`, `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `userId`, `actionType`, `taskName` |  |
| `updateArchiveDelete` | Depends on the body | `task._id`, `task.ParentTaskId` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `sprintId`, `task`, `deletedStatusKey` | `deletedStatusKey` `1` deletes and needs `task.task_delete`, `2` archives and needs `task.task_archive`, `0` restores and needs `task.task_list`. |
| `convertToSubTask` | `task.task_convert_to_subtask`, `task.sub_task_create` | `selectedTaskId`, `taskId` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `sprintId`, `selectedTaskId`, `taskId`, `oldProject`, `isSubTask` |  |
| `convertToTask` | `task.convert_to_task`, `task.task_create` | `taskId`, `parentTaskId`, `projectData.id` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `taskId`, `sprintObj`, `parentTaskId`, `oldSprintObj`, `oldProject` |  |
| `convertToList` | `task.task_convert_to_list`, `project.project_sprint_create` | `taskId`, `projectData.id` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `taskId`, `folderData`, `sprintObj`, `isSubTask` |  |
| `moveTask` | `task.task_move` | `moveTaskId`, `projectData.id` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `sprintObj`, `moveTaskId`, `oldSprintObj`, `oldProject`, `isSubTask`, `assignee`, `watcher` | The key is also needed in the project the task moves to. |
| `mergeTask` | `task.task_merge` | `taskId`, `mergeTaskId` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `taskId`, `mergeTaskId`, `oldProject`, `isSubTask` | The key is also needed in the destination project. |
| `duplicateTask` | `task.task_duplicate` | `selectedTaskId`, `projectData.id` | `companyId`, `projectData`, `sprintObj`, `selectedTaskId`, `oldProject`, `isSubTask`, `duplicateData`, `assignee`, `watcher`, `taskName`, `oldSprintObj` | The key is also needed in the project the copy goes to. |
| `addToList` | `task.task_move` | `taskId`, `sprintId` | `companyId`, `CompanyId`, `taskId`, `sprintId` |  |
| `removeFromList` | `task.task_list` | `taskId`, `sprintId` | `companyId`, `CompanyId`, `taskId`, `sprintId` |  |

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/tasks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "updateStatus",
  "task": { "_id": "<task id>", "sprintId": "<list id>" },
  "newStatus": {
    "status": { "key": 2, "text": "Doing", "type": "active" },
    "statusKey": 2,
    "statusType": "active"
  },
  "prevStatus": { "taskId": "<task id>" },
  "projectData": { "_id": "<project id>" }
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Task updated successfully.",
  "data": { "status": true, "statusText": "Status updated successfully" }
}
```

`data` is what the action itself answered, and differs from action to action. An action that found nothing to change answers `status: false` with its reason.

**Example: rename a task**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/tasks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "updateTaskName",
  "taskData": { "_id": "<task id>", "sprintId": "<list id>" },
  "firebaseObj": { "TaskName": "Write and review the release notes" },
  "obj": {},
  "projectData": { "_id": "<project id>" }
}
```

**Example: add an assignee**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/tasks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "updateAssignee",
  "type": "assigneeAdd",
  "taskData": { "_id": "<task id>", "sprintId": "<list id>" },
  "firebaseObj": { "AssigneeUserId": "<user id>" },
  "projectData": { "_id": "<project id>" }
}
```

**Example: set a custom field's value**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/tasks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "updateTaskCustomField",
  "taskId": "<task id>",
  "customFieldId": "<custom field id>",
  "updateDetail": { "_id": "<custom field id>", "fieldValue": "In review" }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | No `action`, an action that is not accepted, something that is not an id where an id is expected, a key that is not accepted, a value that does not fit the field, or a person named who holds no seat in the workspace. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 404 | The task does not exist or the caller cannot see it. For a move or a copy, the destination project does not exist. |
| 409 | `isUpdateTask` was `false` and the stored task does not hold the value the request names. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List a task's extra lists

`GET /api/v2/tasks/:id/lists` · beta

Lists the other lists a task has been added to, besides its own.

The ids are always given. A list's name and its project's name are given only for a live list the caller can open.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The task must be one the caller can open |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The task. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/tasks/<id>/lists
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Task lists.",
  "data": {
    "taskId": "<task id>",
    "extraLists": [
      {
        "projectId": "<project id>",
        "sprintId": "<list id>",
        "addedBy": "<user id>",
        "addedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
        "name": "Sprint 12",
        "projectName": "Website"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

**Paging**

None.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The task does not exist, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Everything

One list of tasks across every project the caller can read, with filters, grouping, sorting and a cursor, and the caller's saved views of that list.

### List tasks across projects

`POST /api/v2/tasks/everything` · beta

Lists tasks from every project the caller can read, filtered, grouped, sorted and paged.

It is a read sent as a `POST`, so a token needs the `write` scope. Every key of the body is optional, and an unknown key at any level is a 400.

Whatever the body says, the server reads only projects the caller can open and whose task list their role may read, leaves out closed, archived and restricted projects, other people's personal lists, private lists the caller is not on, and deleted or archived tasks.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Accepted, and held to its projects |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `filter.status` | array |  | Up to 100 status names or whole-number keys. Names match exactly. |
| `filter.statusType` | string[] |  | Any of `default_active`, `active`, `done`, `close`, `default_close`. |
| `filter.assignee` | array |  | Up to 100 user ids, or `unassigned`. |
| `filter.priority` | string[] |  | Up to 100 priority names. |
| `filter.dueDate` | object |  | `{ from, to, none }`. `from` and `to` are ISO dates or times in milliseconds. `none: true` means "no due date" and cannot be combined with a range. |
| `filter.taskType` | array |  | Up to 100 task type names or keys. |
| `filter.tags` | string[] |  | Up to 100 tag ids. |
| `filter.search` | string |  | Text of at most 200 characters. |
| `filter.projectIds` | id[] |  | Up to 500 project ids. It can only narrow what the caller may read. |
| `filter.sprintIds` | id[] |  | Up to 100 list ids: tasks that live in one of them or were added to one. A list the caller cannot open is ignored. |
| `group` | string |  | `none`, `status`, `assignee`, `project`, `priority` or `dueDate`. `none` when left out. |
| `sort` | object |  | `{ by, dir }`. `by` is `updatedAt` or `DueDate`; `dir` is `asc` or `desc`. Newest update first when left out; `DueDate` defaults to `asc`. |
| `cursor` | string |  | The `nextCursor` of the page before. |
| `limit` | number |  | A whole number from 1. 50 when left out, and never more than 100. |
| `includeSubtasks` | boolean |  | `false` when left out: top-level tasks only. |
| `includeClosedProjects` | boolean |  | `false` when left out. `true` adds back closed projects only. |
| `timezone` | string |  | An IANA timezone name, used for due-date groups. `UTC` when left out. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/tasks/everything
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "filter": { "statusType": ["default_active", "active"], "assignee": ["<user id>"] },
  "sort": { "by": "DueDate", "dir": "asc" },
  "limit": 50
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Tasks fetched successfully.",
  "data": {
    "rows": [
      {
        "_id": "<task id>",
        "TaskName": "Write the release notes",
        "TaskKey": "WEB-12",
        "status": { "key": 2, "text": "Doing", "type": "active" },
        "statusKey": 2,
        "statusType": "active",
        "Task_Priority": "HIGH",
        "AssigneeUserId": ["<user id>"],
        "DueDate": "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z",
        "ProjectID": "<project id>",
        "sprintId": "<list id>",
        "TaskType": "task",
        "TaskTypeKey": 1,
        "tagsArray": [],
        "subTasks": 0,
        "ancestors": [],
        "ParentTaskId": "",
        "isParentTask": true,
        "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "groups": [{ "key": null, "count": 37 }],
    "nextCursor": "<cursor>",
    "projects": {
      "<project id>": {
        "_id": "<project id>",
        "ProjectName": "Website",
        "ProjectCode": "WEB",
        "taskStatusData": [],
        "taskTypeCounts": [],
        "apps": [],
        "isPersonal": false,
        "edit": { "status": true, "priority": true }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `rows` | Up to `limit` tasks. Each carries `_id`, `TaskName`, `TaskKey`, `status`, `statusKey`, `statusType`, `Task_Priority`, `AssigneeUserId`, `DueDate`, `startDate`, `ProjectID`, `sprintId`, `folderObjId`, `TaskType`, `TaskTypeKey`, `tagsArray`, `subTasks`, `ancestors`, `ParentTaskId`, `isParentTask`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` and `sprintArray` (the list the task lives in, with its `name`). A task that was added to other lists also carries `extraLists`: each entry has `projectId`, `sprintId`, `addedBy` and `addedAt`, and `name` and `projectName` only for a list the caller can open. No custom field values. |
| `groups` | On the first page, a list of `{ key, count }` for the chosen `group`, counted over the same filter. `key` is `null` for "no value". With `group: none` it holds one entry with the total. On a later page it is `null`. |
| `nextCursor` | A string when more rows exist, otherwise `null`. |
| `projects` | A map by project id, for the projects the rows name. Each carries `_id`, `ProjectName`, `ProjectCode`, `projectIcon`, `taskStatusData`, `taskTypeCounts`, `apps`, `statusType`, `isPersonal` and `edit: { status, priority }`, which says whether the caller may change those two fields on that project's tasks. With `group: project` it also names every project counted; one that has no row on the page carries only `_id`, `ProjectName`, `ProjectCode`, `projectIcon`, `statusType` and `isPersonal`. |

**Paging**

Cursor. Send `nextCursor` back as `cursor`. A cursor is good only for the same workspace, person, filter, sort and the two `include` switches; with anything else it is a 400. The page size and the grouping may change between pages. Rows with no value for the sort key come last, in both directions.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | An unknown key, a wrong type, a value out of range, or a cursor that does not fit the request. The body is `{ status: false, statusText: "Request refused", message, field }`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. The person holds no active seat in the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List saved views

`GET /api/v2/tasks/everything/views` · beta

Lists the caller's own saved views of the Everything list, by name.

Any signed-in person with an active seat in the workspace may call it, and it lists only the views that person saved. Answers with `data`, sorted by name, each view with `_id`, `name`, `settings`, `isDefault` and `updatedAt`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/tasks/everything/views
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Views fetched successfully.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<view id>",
      "name": "My open work",
      "settings": {
        "mode": "list",
        "hideDone": true,
        "group": "project",
        "sortBy": "DueDate",
        "sortDir": "asc"
      },
      "isDefault": false,
      "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person holds no active seat in the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Save a view

`POST /api/v2/tasks/everything/views` · beta

Saves a view: a name and the settings of the Everything list.

A view belongs to the person who made it. A person can keep at most 50.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | 1 to 80 characters. |
| `settings` | object | yes | An object with any of: `mode` (`list`, `board`, `table`), `search` (up to 200 characters), `status`, `assignee`, `priority`, `taskType` (lists of up to 100), `projectIds` (up to 500 ids), `due` (`overdue`, `today`, `week`, `later`, `none` or empty), `group` (`none`, `status`, `assignee`, `project`, `priority`, `dueDate`), `sortBy` (`updatedAt`, `DueDate`), `sortDir` (`asc`, `desc`), `showSubtasks`, `hideDone`, `includeClosed` (each `true` or `false`). An unknown key is refused. |
| `isDefault` | boolean |  | Makes this the view the page opens with. Only one view is the default. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/tasks/everything/views
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "My open work",
  "settings": {
    "mode": "list",
    "hideDone": true,
    "group": "project",
    "sortBy": "DueDate",
    "sortDir": "asc"
  }
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "View saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<view id>",
    "name": "My open work",
    "settings": {
      "mode": "list",
      "hideDone": true,
      "group": "project",
      "sortBy": "DueDate",
      "sortDir": "asc"
    },
    "isDefault": false,
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | An unknown key, a missing or malformed field, or a value a setting does not take. The body names the `field`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person holds no active seat in the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a saved view

`PATCH /api/v2/tasks/everything/views/:id` · beta

Changes the name, the settings or the default mark of one of the caller's views.

Any signed-in person with an active seat in the workspace may call it, on a view that person saved; any other id answers 404. Reads `name` (string), `settings` (object) and `isDefault` (boolean) from the body, and changes the ones sent. Making a view the default clears the mark on the caller's other views. Answers with `data`: the view as it is now.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The view. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | 1 to 80 characters. |
| `settings` | object |  | Replaces the stored settings. The same keys as when saving a view. |
| `isDefault` | boolean |  | Makes this the default view, or stops it being the default. |

At least one of the three is required.

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/tasks/everything/views/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "name": "My open work, by due date" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "View saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<view id>",
    "name": "My open work, by due date",
    "settings": {
      "mode": "list",
      "hideDone": true,
      "group": "project",
      "sortBy": "DueDate",
      "sortDir": "asc"
    },
    "isDefault": false,
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | An unknown key, a missing or malformed field, or a value a setting does not take. The body names the `field`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person holds no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The view does not exist, or is someone else's. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Delete a saved view

`DELETE /api/v2/tasks/everything/views/:id` · beta

Deletes one of the caller's views.

Any signed-in person with an active seat in the workspace may call it, on a view that person saved; any other id answers 404. The view is marked deleted and leaves the list, and its default mark is cleared. Answers with `statusText` `View deleted.` and no `data`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The view. |

**Request**

```http
DELETE /api/v2/tasks/everything/views/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "View deleted." }
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person holds no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The view does not exist, or is someone else's. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Projects

Duplicating a project, and saving a project as a template to start others from. To list projects, use `GET /api/public-v1/projects`.

### Duplicate a project

`POST /api/v2/projects/:id/duplicate` · beta

Copies a project: its settings, folders, lists and automations, and its tasks when asked.

The copy gets a new project code. Automations are copied switched off. Custom fields are shared with the copy, not duplicated. A personal list cannot be duplicated.

Up to 300 tasks are copied before the answer. More than that are copied afterwards as a job: the answer carries the job, and `GET /api/v2/projects/:id/duplicate` on the copy reports how far it has got.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | `project.project_create`. The project must be one the caller can open |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The project to copy. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | The copy's name, at most 255 characters. |
| `include.tasks` | boolean | yes | Copy the tasks. |
| `include.assignees` | boolean | yes | Keep the assignees of the copied tasks. |
| `include.dates` | boolean | yes | Keep the dates of the project, its lists and its tasks. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/projects/<id>/duplicate
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Website (copy)",
  "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": false }
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Project duplicated.",
  "data": {
    "project": { "_id": "<new project id>", "ProjectName": "Website (copy)", "ProjectCode": "WEB2" },
    "counts": { "folders": 2, "lists": 5, "tasks": 48, "automations": 3 },
    "notes": [{ "code": "automations_disabled", "count": 3 }],
    "sharedFields": ["<custom field id>"],
    "job": null
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `project` | The whole stored copy; the example shows some of its fields. |
| `counts` | What was copied. `tasks` is `0` when the tasks are being copied as a job. |
| `notes` | What was left out or changed, each with a `code` and a `count` or a `name`: `tasks_left_out`, `private_lists_left`, `automations_skipped`, `automations_disabled`, `subfolder_moved_to_top`. |
| `sharedFields` | The ids of the custom fields now shared with the copy. |
| `job` | `null`, or `{ id, status, total, processed, created }` when the tasks are copied after the answer. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is missing `name` or `include`, carries another key, a flag is not `true` or `false`, or the project is a personal list. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 404 | The project does not exist, is in the trash, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a duplicate's progress

`GET /api/v2/projects/:id/duplicate` · beta

Reports how far the tasks of a large copy have got.

Needs a project the caller can open. The path id is the copy that a `POST /api/v2/projects/:id/duplicate` made. Answers with `data`: the newest copy job that the caller started for that project, as `id`, `status`, `total`, `processed` and `created`, or `null` when there is none.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The project must be one the caller can open |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The copy, not the project it was copied from. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/projects/<id>/duplicate
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Duplicate progress fetched.",
  "data": {
    "id": "<job id>",
    "status": "processing",
    "total": 1200,
    "processed": 400,
    "created": 400
  }
}
```

`status` is `processing`, `done` or `failed`. `data` is `null` when the caller started no copy job for that project.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The project does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Save a project as a template

`POST /api/v2/projects/:id/template` · beta

Saves a project's settings, folders, lists and, when asked, its tasks and automations as a template.

The template keeps no ids of the project it came from. Tasks start in the project's first status, with nothing ticked. A template holds at most 2000 tasks and a workspace at most 100 templates. A personal list cannot be saved.

A template is offered to everyone unless the project has a private list or `everyone` says otherwise. A template offered to everyone leaves private lists out. Automations are kept only when the caller may manage automations.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | `project.project_create`. The project must be one the caller can open |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The project to save. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | The template's name, at most 255 characters. |
| `include.tasks` | boolean | yes | Keep the tasks. |
| `include.assignees` | boolean | yes | Keep the assignees. |
| `include.dates` | boolean | yes | Keep the dates, kept as distances from the project's start. |
| `include.automations` | boolean | yes | Keep the automations. |
| `description` | string |  | Up to 2000 characters. |
| `everyone` | boolean |  | Offer the template to everyone who can create projects. Defaults to `true` unless the project holds a private list. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/projects/<id>/template
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Website launch",
  "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": true, "automations": false }
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Template saved.",
  "data": {
    "template": {
      "_id": "<template id>",
      "name": "Website launch",
      "description": "",
      "everyone": true,
      "sourcePrivate": false,
      "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": true, "automations": false },
      "counts": { "folders": 2, "lists": 5, "tasks": 48, "automations": 0 },
      "statuses": [{ "key": "default_active", "name": "To do", "type": "default_active" }],
      "createdBy": "<user id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "canManage": true
    },
    "notes": [{ "code": "private_lists_left", "count": 1 }]
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `template` | The saved template. |
| `notes` | What was left out or changed, each with a `code` and a `count` or a `name`: `tasks_left_out`, `private_lists_left`, `automations_skipped`, `subfolder_moved_to_top`. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is missing `name` or `include`, carries another key, has a value of the wrong type, or the project is a personal list; the project holds more than 2000 tasks and `include.tasks` is `true`; or the workspace already holds 100 templates. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 404 | The project does not exist, is in the trash, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List project templates

`GET /api/v2/projects/templates` · beta

Lists the project templates the caller may use, by name.

Owners and admins see every template. Anyone else sees the templates offered to everyone and the ones they saved.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | `project.project_create` |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/projects/templates
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Templates fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<template id>",
      "name": "Website launch",
      "description": "",
      "everyone": true,
      "sourcePrivate": false,
      "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": true, "automations": false },
      "counts": { "folders": 2, "lists": 5, "tasks": 48, "automations": 0 },
      "statuses": [{ "key": "default_active", "name": "To do", "type": "default_active" }],
      "createdBy": "<user id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "canManage": true
    }
  ]
}
```

`data` is the template as the caller may see it; `canManage` says whether the caller may change or delete it.

**Paging**

None. A workspace holds at most 100 templates.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Create a project from a template

`POST /api/v2/projects/templates/:id/use` · beta

Creates a project from a template, with its folders, lists and, when asked, its tasks.

The caller becomes a member of the new project. People who have left the workspace, and statuses and custom fields that no longer exist, are dropped. Dates are laid out from `startDate`; without it the project and its tasks have no dates.

The answer is the one of [Duplicate a project](#duplicate-a-project).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | `project.project_create` |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The template. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | The project's name, at most 255 characters. |
| `include.tasks` | boolean | yes | Use the tasks. |
| `include.assignees` | boolean | yes | Use the assignees. |
| `include.dates` | boolean | yes | Use the dates, kept as distances from the project's start. |
| `include.automations` | boolean | yes | Use the automations. |
| `code` | string |  | The project key, letters and digits, at most 10. It must not be taken. Defaults to a generated key. |
| `isPrivate` | boolean |  | Make the project private. Defaults to the template's own setting. |
| `startDate` | date |  | The project's first day. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/projects/templates/<id>/use
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Website relaunch",
  "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": true, "automations": false },
  "startDate": "2026-11-02"
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Project created from the template.",
  "data": {
    "project": {
      "_id": "<new project id>",
      "ProjectName": "Website relaunch",
      "ProjectCode": "WEB3"
    },
    "counts": { "folders": 2, "lists": 5, "tasks": 48, "automations": 0 },
    "notes": [],
    "sharedFields": [],
    "job": null
  }
}
```

`data` has the fields described under [Duplicate a project](#duplicate-a-project).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is missing `name` or `include`, carries another key, has a value of the wrong type, or `code` is already taken. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The person's role lacks the permission the route needs. |
| 404 | The template does not exist, or is not one the caller may use. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a project template

`PATCH /api/v2/projects/templates/:id` · beta

Changes a template's name, description or whether it is offered to everyone.

A template that holds a private list cannot be offered to everyone. Offering a template to everyone is for the person who saved it; owners and admins may also do it for one saved from a public project.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The person who saved the template, an owner or an admin |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The template. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | At most 255 characters. |
| `description` | string |  | Up to 2000 characters. |
| `everyone` | boolean |  | Offer the template to everyone who can create projects. |

Name at least one key. Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/projects/templates/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "name": "Website launch v2" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Template updated.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<template id>",
    "name": "Website launch",
    "description": "",
    "everyone": true,
    "sourcePrivate": false,
    "include": { "tasks": true, "assignees": false, "dates": true, "automations": false },
    "counts": { "folders": 2, "lists": 5, "tasks": 48, "automations": 0 },
    "statuses": [{ "key": "default_active", "name": "To do", "type": "default_active" }],
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "canManage": true
  }
}
```

`data` is the template as the caller may see it; `canManage` says whether the caller may change or delete it.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body names nothing to change, carries another key, has a value of the wrong type, or offers a template that holds a private list to everyone. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The template does not exist, or is not one the caller may use. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Delete a project template

`DELETE /api/v2/projects/templates/:id` · beta

Deletes a template and the task rows it kept. Projects made from it are not affected.

Allowed for the person who saved the template and for owners and admins; a template the caller may not see answers 404 and one they may see but not manage answers 403. The template is marked deleted and so are the task rows kept with it. Answers with `statusText` `Template deleted.` and no `data`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The person who saved the template, an owner or an admin |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The template. |

**Request**

```http
DELETE /api/v2/projects/templates/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "Template deleted." }
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The template does not exist, or is not one the caller may use. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Pages

Docs and wiki pages, their version history, and who a comment on one can name. A page belongs to a project or to the workspace. A private page is its author's alone; any other page is open to whoever can see its project. A page the caller cannot see answers the same as one that does not exist.

### List pages

`GET /api/v2/pages` · beta

Lists pages, without their bodies.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. It reads `projectId` (id), `taskId` (id, which wins over `projectId`) and `scope` (`all`, `shared` or `trash`) from the query; with none of them it lists the workspace's pages that belong to no project. A malformed id answers a failed status. Only pages the caller may read are listed: a private page is its author's, a project's pages go to those who can see the project, and a page shared with the caller by name is included except in the trash. Answers with `data`: the pages ordered by `order`, each with an `excerpt` of up to 160 characters in place of its body.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id |  | The pages of that project. |
| `taskId` | id |  | The pages linked to that task. It wins over `projectId`. |
| `scope` | string |  | `all` lists every page the caller can see. `trash` lists the deleted ones. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/pages?projectId=<project id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Pages fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<page id>",
      "title": "Release checklist",
      "ProjectID": "<project id>",
      "visibility": "project",
      "isWiki": false,
      "linkedTasks": [],
      "createdBy": "<user id>",
      "updatedBy": "<user id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "order": 1759311000000,
      "excerpt": "Before every release, check that",
      "reviewState": "none"
    }
  ]
}
```

With no parameter, the list holds the workspace's own pages: the ones that belong to no project.

`excerpt` is the first 160 characters of the page's text. `reviewState` is `none`, `verified`, `due` or `stale`.

**Paging**

None. The whole list is returned, in the pages' own order.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `projectId` or `taskId` is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Create a page

`POST /api/v2/pages` · beta

Creates a page in a project, or in the workspace when no project is named.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, except the guest role, which cannot add pages; a page in a project also needs edit rights on that project (403 without them, 404 when the project cannot be seen), and a parent page needs edit rights too. Reads `title` (string, required), `projectId`, `parentPageId` (ids), `visibility` (string), `linkedTasks` (id list of tasks the caller can open), `contentBlocks` (list), `isWiki` (boolean), `ownerId` (id) and `reviewDate` from the body. Answers with `data`: the saved page.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | With a `projectId`, the caller must be able to edit that project |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | yes | Up to 200 characters. |
| `projectId` | id |  | The project the page belongs to. |
| `parentPageId` | id |  | The page this one sits under. |
| `contentBlocks` | array |  | The body, as a list of blocks: `{ type, data }`. Types include `header`, `paragraph`, `list`, `checklist`, `code`, `quote`, `table`, `callout`, `delimiter`, `image` and `embed`. The body can be at most 512 KB. |
| `visibility` | string |  | `project` (the default) or `private`. |
| `linkedTasks` | id[] |  | Tasks the page is attached to. Each must be a task the caller can open. |
| `isWiki` | boolean |  | Marks the page as a wiki page, which has an owner and a review date. |
| `ownerId` | id |  | The wiki page's owner. The caller when left out. |
| `reviewDate` | date |  | When the wiki page is next due for review. Three months from now when left out. |
| `createdByAgent` | boolean |  | Marks the page as a draft that a person approves. A page an agent token creates is always one, under the token's own name. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/pages
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Release checklist",
  "projectId": "<project id>",
  "contentBlocks": [
    { "type": "header", "data": { "text": "Before every release", "level": 2 } },
    { "type": "paragraph", "data": { "text": "Check that the changelog is up to date." } }
  ]
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Page created.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "visibility": "project",
    "isWiki": false,
    "linkedTasks": [],
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "updatedBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "content": {
      "html": "<h2>Before every release</h2><p>Check that the changelog is up to date.</p>",
      "blocks": { "time": 1759311000000, "blocks": [], "version": "2.30.7" }
    },
    "rawText": "Before every release Check that the changelog is up to date."
  }
}
```

`data` is the stored page. `content.blocks.blocks` holds the blocks that were sent; the example leaves them out.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | No title, a title over 200 characters, an id that is not an id, a review date that is not a date, or a body that is too large. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The project or the parent page does not exist, or the caller may not use it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a page

`GET /api/v2/pages/:id` · beta

Returns one page with its body.

Any signed-in member of the workspace who may read the page. A page the caller may not read answers the same way as one that does not exist, with a failed status and `Page not found.`. Answers with `data`: the page with its body, and the flags `canEdit`, `canChangeProperties` and `canManageShares` as they apply to the caller.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/pages/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Page fetched.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "visibility": "project",
    "isWiki": false,
    "linkedTasks": [],
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "updatedBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "content": {
      "html": "<h2>Before every release</h2>",
      "blocks": {
        "time": 1759311000000,
        "blocks": [{ "type": "header", "data": { "text": "Before every release", "level": 2 } }],
        "version": "2.30.7"
      }
    },
    "rawText": "Before every release",
    "reviewState": "none"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a page

`PUT /api/v2/pages/:id` · beta

Changes a page's title, body or properties. Only the keys sent are changed.

When the title or the body changes, the state before the change is kept as a version.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the page |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string |  | Up to 200 characters. |
| `contentBlocks` | array |  | The new body, as a list of blocks. |
| `contentHtml` | string |  | The new body, as HTML. Send one of the two. |
| `visibility` | string |  | `project` or `private`. Only the author can make a page private. |
| `linkedTasks` | id[] |  | Replaces the tasks the page is attached to. A task added here must be one the caller can open. |
| `isWiki` | boolean |  | Marks or unmarks the page as a wiki page. |
| `ownerId` | id |  | The wiki page's owner. |
| `reviewDate` | date |  | When the wiki page is next due for review. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/pages/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "title": "Release checklist (2026)" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Page saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist (2026)",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "visibility": "project",
    "isWiki": false,
    "linkedTasks": [],
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "updatedBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the whole stored page after the change; the example shows some of its fields.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A title over 200 characters, an id that is not an id, a review date that is not a date, or a body that is too large. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. Someone other than the author tried to make the page private. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller may not change it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Delete a page

`DELETE /api/v2/pages/:id` · beta

Moves a page to the trash, together with the pages under it that the caller can see.

Allowed for the author of a private page and for owners and admins; for a page in a project, for those with edit rights on that project; for a workspace page, for any member. The guest role cannot delete pages. Answers 404 when the page cannot be seen and 403 when it can be seen but not deleted. Answers with `data.deleted`: the number of pages moved to the trash.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | A private page goes only by its author, an owner or an admin. Any other page needs edit rights on its project |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Request**

```http
DELETE /api/v2/pages/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "Page deleted.", "data": { "deleted": 3 } }
```

`deleted` counts the page and the pages under it that went with it.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller can see the page's project but may not edit it. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Restore a page

`PUT /api/v2/pages/:id/restore` · beta

Takes a page back out of the trash. Only the page itself: the pages that went with it are restored one by one.

Needs edit rights on the page as reached without a share by name, and the guest role is excluded. The page must be in the trash, else the answer is a failed status with `Page not found in trash.`. Answers with `data`: the restored page.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the page |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/pages/<id>/restore
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Page restored.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist",
    "ProjectID": "<project id>",
    "visibility": "project",
    "isWiki": false,
    "linkedTasks": [],
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "updatedBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page is not in the trash, or the caller may not change it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List a page's versions

`GET /api/v2/pages/:id/versions` · beta

Lists the versions kept for a page, newest first, without their bodies.
