Any signed-in member of the workspace who may read the page; a page the caller may not read answers 404 `Page not found.`. Answers with `data`: the versions the caller may see, newest first, without their bodies.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/pages/<id>/versions
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Versions fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<version id>",
      "pageId": "<page id>",
      "title": "Release checklist",
      "name": "Before the rewrite",
      "reason": "manual",
      "savedBy": "<user id>",
      "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "visibility": "project"
    }
  ]
}
```

`reason` says why the version was kept: `manual` (someone saved it), `author` (someone else was about to change the page), `interval` (ten minutes had passed since the last version), `restore` (the state before a restore) or `legacy`. `name` is empty for a version nobody named. A version kept while the page was private is shown only to the page's author.

**Paging**

None. A page keeps at most 100 unnamed and 30 named versions.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Save a version

`POST /api/v2/pages/:id/versions` · beta

Keeps the page as it is now as a version, with a name when one is given.

Needs edit rights on the page. Reads `name` (string, up to 80 characters, optional) from the body. A page with no content answers 400, a page too large to keep answers 400, and a page may hold a limited number of named versions. When the latest version already matches the page, no new version is made and the answer names or returns that one. Answers with `data`: the version.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the page |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | A name for the version, up to 80 characters. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/pages/<id>/versions
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "name": "Before the rewrite" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Version saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<version id>",
    "pageId": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist",
    "name": "Before the rewrite",
    "reason": "manual",
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "visibility": "project"
  }
}
```

When the newest version already holds this state, no new version is made: the answer is that version, named if a name was sent.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The name is not text or is over 80 characters, the page is empty, the page already has 30 named versions, or the page is too large to keep. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller can see the page but may not change it. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a version

`GET /api/v2/pages/:id/versions/:versionId` · beta

Returns one version with its body.

Any signed-in member of the workspace who may read the page and that version; otherwise 404 with `Version not found.` or `Page not found.`. Answers with `data`: the version with its title and body.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |
| `versionId` | id | The version. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/pages/<id>/versions/<versionId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Version fetched.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<version id>",
    "pageId": "<page id>",
    "title": "Release checklist",
    "name": "Before the rewrite",
    "reason": "manual",
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "visibility": "project",
    "blocks": [{ "type": "header", "data": { "text": "Before every release", "level": 2 } }],
    "rawText": "Before every release"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page or the version does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Name a version

`PUT /api/v2/pages/:id/versions/:versionId` · beta

Names a version, or clears its name.

Needs edit rights on the page. Reads `name` (string, up to 80 characters) from the body; an empty name clears it, and naming one more version than the limit allows answers 400. Answers with `data`: the version.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the page |

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |
| `versionId` | id | The version. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | The name, up to 80 characters. An empty name clears it. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/pages/<id>/versions/<versionId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "name": "Signed off" }

{

    "name": "Signed off",
    "reason": "manual",
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "visibility": "project"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The name is not text or is over 80 characters, or the page already has 30 named versions. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller can see the page but may not change it. |
| 404 | The page or the version does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

`POST /api/v2/pages/:id/versions/:versionId/restore` · beta

Puts a version's title and body back on the page. The page as it was is kept as a version first.

`data` is the whole stored page after the restore; the example shows some of its fields.

**Errors**

|---|---|

| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller can see the page but may not change it. |
| 404 | The page or the version does not exist, or the caller cannot see it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List who can be mentioned on a page

`GET /api/v2/pages/:id/comments/people` · beta

Lists the ids of the people a comment on a page may mention or be assigned to.

These are the active members who can read the page.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to read the page |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/pages/<id>/comments/people
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "People fetched.", "data": ["<user id>"] }
```

**Paging**

None.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Assign a page comment

`PUT /api/v2/pages/:id/comments/:commentId/assign` · beta

Assigns a comment thread to a person, or clears the assignment.

Assigning a thread reopens it. The assignee is told once, unless they assigned it to themselves.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to read the page. Once a thread is assigned, only its author, the person who assigned it, its assignee, an owner or an admin can change the assignment |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |
| `commentId` | id | The comment. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `assigneeId` | id |  | An active member who can read the page. Empty, or left out, clears the assignment. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/pages/<id>/comments/<commentId>/assign
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "assigneeId": "<user id>" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Comment assigned.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<comment id>",
    "pageId": "<page id>",
    "userId": "<user id>",
    "message": "Can we cut this section?",
    "blockId": "",
    "mentionIds": [],
    "assigneeId": "<user id>",
    "assignedBy": "<user id>",
    "assignedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "resolved": false,
    "reactions": [{ "emoji": "👍", "userId": "<user id>", "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z" }],
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the whole stored comment; the example shows some of its fields.

Clearing the assignment answers `Comment unassigned.`.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The comment is a reply (only a thread can be assigned), or `assigneeId` is not an active member who can read the page. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. Or the comment does not exist on it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### React to a page comment

`PUT /api/v2/pages/:id/comments/:commentId/reaction` · beta

Puts the caller's reaction on a comment, or takes it off if it is already there.

A reaction does not count as an edit: the comment's `updatedAt` stays as it was.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to read the page |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The page. |
| `commentId` | id | The comment. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `emoji` | string | yes | One of 👍, ❤️, 😄, 🎉, 😮, 😢, 🚀 or 👀. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/pages/<id>/comments/<commentId>/reaction
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "emoji": "👍" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Reaction added.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<comment id>",
    "pageId": "<page id>",
    "userId": "<user id>",
    "message": "Can we cut this section?",
    "blockId": "",
    "mentionIds": [],
    "assigneeId": "<user id>",
    "assignedBy": "<user id>",
    "assignedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "resolved": false,
    "reactions": [{ "emoji": "👍", "userId": "<user id>", "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z" }],
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the whole stored comment; the example shows some of its fields.

Taking a reaction off answers `Reaction removed.`.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `emoji` is not one of the supported reactions. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The page does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. Or the comment does not exist on it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Agents

Read-only views of the workspace's AI agents: who they are, what they are running and what they have proposed. Runs and proposals are narrowed to the projects and tasks the caller can read.

### List agents

`GET /api/v2/agents` · beta

Lists the workspace's agents, oldest first.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data`: the agents that are not deleted, oldest first. Each agent's `projectIds` list is cut to the projects the caller can open, unless the caller is an owner or admin, and `projectScoped` says whether the agent is limited to projects at all.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/agents
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Agents fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<agent id>",
      "name": "Triage",
      "autonomy": 1,
      "paused": false,
      "skills": ["<skill key>"],
      "spendCapUsd": 20
    }
  ]
}
```

Each row is the whole stored agent; the example shows some of its fields.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read the team board

`GET /api/v2/agents/team` · beta

Returns the people and agents of the workspace, what each is on right now, and a short standup.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `hoursPerWeek` (a number above 0, 40 when left out) from the query. Answers with `data`: the people and agents of the workspace with what each is on, their load for the week, time off, and a `standup` composed from them.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hoursPerWeek` | number |  | The weekly capacity each person's load is measured against. 40 when left out. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/agents/team
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Team board fetched.",
  "data": {
    "people": [
      {
        "kind": "person",
        "id": "<user id>",
        "name": "Sample Person",
        "email": "person@example.com",
        "roleType": 3,
        "online": true,
        "timer": null,
        "nowOn": "Write the release notes",
        "openTasks": 4,
        "loggedHours": 12,
        "capacityHours": 40,
        "load": 30,
        "pto": null,
        "status": "available"
      }
    ],
    "agents": [
      {
        "kind": "agent",
        "id": "<agent id>",
        "name": "Triage",
        "ownerId": "<user id>",
        "autonomy": 1,
        "paused": false,
        "skills": ["<skill key>"],
        "allowedActions": [],
        "spend": { "usd": 1.4, "cap": 20, "runs": 9 },
        "run": null,
        "nowOn": "",
        "status": "idle"
      }
    ],
    "activity": [
      {
        "at": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
        "kind": "agent",
        "who": "Triage",
        "what": "done",
        "taskId": "<task id>",
        "runId": "<run id>",
        "status": "done"
      }
    ],
    "totals": {
      "people": 1,
      "agents": 1,
      "running": 0,
      "away": 0,
      "load": 30,
      "weekStart": "2026-09-28T00:00:00.000Z"
    },
    "standup": {
      "generatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "lines": [
        "Sample Person: next up \"Write the release notes\" · 4 open tasks · 12h logged this week"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

A person's `status` is `working`, `available`, `away` or `offline`; an agent's is `running`, `paused` or `idle`. Hours and load are `null` for a caller who may not see other people's time. A task the caller cannot read is shown as hidden, without its name.

The example shows some of the fields of each row.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List agent runs

`GET /api/v2/agents/runs` · beta

Lists agent runs, newest first, with a summary of the runs that are open now.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `status`, `projectId`, `agentId`, `taskId`, `errorType` and `limit` from the query; an `errorType` outside the known provider errors answers 400. Owners and admins see every project's runs, and other members see runs in the projects they can open, except runs on tasks in private lists they are not on. Answers with `data` (the runs, newest first) and `summary` (the runs open now).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | string |  | `open` (queued, running or waiting for approval), or one of `queued`, `running`, `waiting_approval`, `done`, `skipped`, `failed`, `stopped`. |
| `projectId` | id |  | Runs in that project. |
| `agentId` | id |  | Runs of that agent. |
| `taskId` | id |  | Runs on that task. |
| `errorType` | string |  | Failed runs with that kind of provider error. |
| `limit` | number |  | How many runs to return, from 1 to 200. 50 when left out. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/agents/runs?status=open&limit=20
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Runs fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<run id>",
      "agentId": "<agent id>",
      "agentName": "Triage",
      "taskId": "<task id>",
      "projectId": "<project id>",
      "trigger": "manual",
      "status": "done",
      "startedBy": "<user id>",
      "startedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "finishedAt": "2026-10-01T09:31:10.000Z",
      "elapsedMs": 70000,
      "spend": { "tokens": 1840, "usd": 0.02 }
    }
  ],
  "summary": {
    "running": 1,
    "waitingApproval": 0,
    "agents": 1,
    "elapsedMs": 42000,
    "spendUsd": 0.01,
    "runs": [
      {
        "_id": "<run id>",
        "agentId": "<agent id>",
        "agentName": "Triage",
        "status": "running",
        "taskId": "<task id>",
        "startedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
        "projectId": "<project id>",
        "skill": null,
        "spendUsd": 0.01
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

`summary` sits beside `data`. Each row of `data` is the whole stored run; the example shows some of its fields.

**Paging**

Limit only. The newest runs come first and there is no next page; narrow with the filters.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `errorType` is not one of the known kinds. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read an agent run

`GET /api/v2/agents/runs/:id` · beta

Returns one run with its steps, the audit rows of what it did, and the agent revision it ran on.

Any signed-in member of the workspace who may see the run: for most runs, the people who can open its project and its tasks, and for a run that produced a report, only the person who started it and owners and admins. A run the caller may not see answers 404 as one that does not exist does. Answers with `data`: the `run` with its steps, the `audit` rows of what it did, the `revision` of the agent it ran on, and a `trace`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The run. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/agents/runs/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Run fetched.",
  "data": {
    "run": {
      "_id": "<run id>",
      "agentId": "<agent id>",
      "agentName": "Triage",
      "taskId": "<task id>",
      "projectId": "<project id>",
      "trigger": "manual",
      "status": "done",
      "startedBy": "<user id>",
      "startedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "finishedAt": "2026-10-01T09:31:10.000Z",
      "elapsedMs": 70000,
      "spend": { "tokens": 1840, "usd": 0.02 },
      "steps": [],
      "agentRevision": 2,
      "undoable": false,
      "undoReason": ""
    },
    "audit": [],
    "revision": { "n": 2, "state": "live" },
    "trace": {}
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `run` | The stored run, with `steps`, the `agentRevision` it was pinned to, and whether it can still be undone (`undoable`, `undoUntil`, `undoReason`). |
| `audit` | Up to 200 audit rows the run wrote, oldest first. |
| `revision` | The agent revision the run used. |
| `trace` | The run's steps and audit rows arranged for display. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The run does not exist, or the caller cannot see its task. A report run is open only to the person it was made for and to owners and admins. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List agent proposals

`GET /api/v2/agents/proposals` · beta

Lists the changes agents have proposed and are waiting on a person for, newest first.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `status` (`pending` when left out, or `all`, or one status), `bucket`, `agentId` and `limit` from the query. A member sees the proposals of projects they can open, plus ones asked for by their own agents, and owners and admins see every project's. Answers with `data` (the proposals, newest first) and `counts`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | string |  | `pending` when left out. `all`, or one of `pending`, `applying`, `approved`, `edited`, `declined`, `undone`, `failed`. |
| `bucket` | string |  | `primary` or `later`. |
| `agentId` | id |  | Proposals of that agent. |
| `limit` | number |  | How many to return, up to 500. 100 when left out. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/agents/proposals
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Proposals fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<proposal id>",
      "agentId": "<agent id>",
      "agentName": "Triage",
      "runId": "<run id>",
      "taskId": "<task id>",
      "projectId": "<project id>",
      "what": "Set the priority to High",
      "why": "The customer reported an outage.",
      "changes": [],
      "status": "pending",
      "bucket": "primary",
      "undoAvailable": false,
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ],
  "counts": {
    "waiting": 1,
    "doneByAi": 0,
    "declined": 0,
    "undone": 0,
    "failed": 0,
    "primary": 1,
    "later": 0
  }
}
```

`counts` sits beside `data` and is counted over everything the caller can read, whatever `status` was asked for.

**Paging**

Limit only. The newest proposals come first and there is no next page.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Dashboards

The answers of the Ask cards on a dashboard. These two routes sit under `/api/v1`; they are described here because a token can call them.

### Read an Ask card's answer

`GET /api/v1/ai/ask/card/:dashboardId/:cardUid` · beta

Returns the answer kept for the caller on one Ask card of a dashboard.

An answer is built from what its asker can open, so each viewer of a dashboard has their own. This never returns anyone else's.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dashboardId` | id | The dashboard. |
| `cardUid` | string | The card's uid on that dashboard. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v1/ai/ask/card/<dashboardId>/<cardUid>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "data": {
    "stored": {
      "question": "What is overdue this week?",
      "projectId": "",
      "answer": "Two tasks are overdue [WEB-12] [WEB-15].",
      "cited": [
        {
          "kind": "task",
          "id": "<task id>",
          "ref": "WEB-12",
          "title": "Write the release notes",
          "project": "Website",
          "projectId": "<project id>",
          "available": true
        }
      ],
      "askedAt": 1759311000000
    },
    "stale": false,
    "refreshDue": false
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `stored` | `null` when the caller has no kept answer for the card. `askedAt` is a time in milliseconds. `cited` lists only the sources the caller can still open. |
| `stale` | The answer is older than the card's refresh setting. |
| `refreshDue` | The answer is stale and nothing has asked for it again within that time. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The card does not exist, is not an Ask card, or the caller cannot open its dashboard. The body carries `code: "card_not_found"`. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Ask an Ask card's question

`POST /api/v1/ai/ask/card/:dashboardId/:cardUid` · beta

Asks a question as the caller and keeps the answer for the caller on that card.

The answer is written by the workspace's AI model from the tasks and pages the caller can open, and counts against the workspace's AI budget.

A kept answer to the same question and project is handed back instead of asking again, unless `fresh` is `true` or the kept answer is past the card's refresh setting.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dashboardId` | id | The dashboard. |
| `cardUid` | string | The card's uid on that dashboard. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `question` | string | yes | The question. Text past 500 characters is cut off. |
| `projectId` | id |  | Limits the answer to one project. |
| `fresh` | boolean |  | `true` asks again even when an answer is kept. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v1/ai/ask/card/<dashboardId>/<cardUid>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "question": "What is overdue this week?" }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "OK",
  "data": {
    "configured": true,
    "mode": "ask",
    "answer": "Two tasks are overdue [WEB-12] [WEB-15].",
    "cited": [
      {
        "kind": "task",
        "id": "<task id>",
        "ref": "WEB-12",
        "title": "Write the release notes",
        "project": "Website",
        "projectId": "<project id>",
        "detail": "Doing · HIGH",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "sources": [],
    "scope": { "projects": 4, "privileged": false },
    "usage": { "tokens": 1250, "model": "<model name>" },
    "askedAt": 1759311000000
  }
}
```

`sources` lists everything that was searched, in the shape of `cited`; the example leaves it empty. `cited` are the sources the answer refers to.

When a kept answer is handed back, `data` is `{ configured: true, question, projectId, answer, cited, askedAt, kept: true }`. When no AI model is configured, `data.configured` is `false`, `answer` is empty and nothing is kept.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | No question. The body carries `code: "question_required"`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The card does not exist, is not an Ask card, or the caller cannot open its dashboard. The body carries `code: "card_not_found"`. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Webhooks

Managing the webhooks described under [Webhook deliveries](#webhook-deliveries) above. A webhook belongs to the person who created it: nobody else can list, change or delete it, or read its delivery log.

### List webhook events

`GET /api/v2/webhooks/events` · beta

Lists the events a webhook can subscribe to.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data`: the list of event names a webhook can subscribe to.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/webhooks/events
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Webhook events.",
  "data": ["task.created", "task.updated", "task.deleted", "task.archived", "task.restored"]
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List webhooks

`GET /api/v2/webhooks` · beta

Lists the caller's own webhooks. The signing secret is never returned here.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data`: the webhooks the caller created, together with any recorded with no owner, each with `name`, `url`, `events`, `active`, `format`, `lastStatus`, `lastDeliveredAt`, `needsAttention` and `createdAt`, and never the secret.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/webhooks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Webhooks fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<webhook id>",
      "name": "Release channel",
      "url": "https://hooks.example.com/alianhub",
      "events": ["task.created", "task.updated"],
      "active": true,
      "format": "json",
      "createdBy": "<user id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "lastStatus": 200,
      "lastDeliveredAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "needsAttention": ""
    }
  ]
}
```

**Paging**

None. The whole list is returned.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Create a webhook

`POST /api/v2/webhooks` · beta

Creates a webhook and returns its signing secret, once.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `name` (string), `url` (string), `events` (string list) and `format` (string) from the body. The address must be an `http` or `https` one that resolves to a public host, or to a private host the person who runs the server allows. Failures answer a failed status with the reason. Answers with `data`: the webhook and its `secret`, shown once.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | Up to 80 characters. |
| `url` | string | yes | An `http` or `https` address on a public host. |
| `events` | string[] | yes | At least one event, or `*` for all of them. |
| `format` | string |  | `json` (the default), `slack` or `discord`. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/webhooks
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Release channel",
  "url": "https://hooks.example.com/alianhub",
  "events": ["task.created", "task.updated"]
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Webhook created. Store the secret now — it is not shown again.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<webhook id>",
    "name": "Release channel",
    "url": "https://hooks.example.com/alianhub",
    "events": ["task.created", "task.updated"],
    "active": true,
    "format": "json",
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "secret": "<signing secret>"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | No name, a name over 80 characters, an address that is not `http` or `https` or does not resolve to a public host, no events, an unknown event, or an unknown format. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a webhook

`PUT /api/v2/webhooks/:id` · beta

Changes a webhook, or switches it on or off. Only the keys sent are changed.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, on a webhook the caller created or one recorded with no owner; any other id answers `Webhook not found.`. Reads `name`, `url`, `events`, `format` and `active` from the body and changes the ones sent; a body with none of them answers `Nothing to update.`. A new `url` is checked as when creating. Answers with `data`: the webhook as it is now.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The webhook. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | Up to 80 characters. |
| `url` | string |  | An `http` or `https` address on a public host. |
| `events` | string[] |  | Replaces the events. |
| `format` | string |  | `json`, `slack` or `discord`. |
| `active` | boolean |  | `false` stops deliveries; `true` starts them again. |

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/webhooks/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "active": false }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Webhook updated.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<webhook id>",
    "name": "Release channel",
    "url": "https://hooks.example.com/alianhub",
    "events": ["task.created", "task.updated"],
    "active": false,
    "format": "json",
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id, a value is not accepted, or the body names nothing to change. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The webhook does not exist, or is someone else's. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Delete a webhook

`DELETE /api/v2/webhooks/:id` · beta

Deletes a webhook, its delivery log and its signing secret.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, on a webhook the caller created or one recorded with no owner; any other id answers `Webhook not found.`. Removes the webhook, its delivery log and its signing secret. Answers with `statusText` `Webhook deleted.` and no `data`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The webhook. |

**Request**

```http
DELETE /api/v2/webhooks/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "Webhook deleted." }
```

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The webhook does not exist, or is someone else's. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a webhook's delivery log

`GET /api/v2/webhooks/:id/logs` · beta

Lists a webhook's deliveries, newest first.

Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, on a webhook the caller created or one recorded with no owner; any other id answers `Webhook not found.`. Reads `limit` (1 to 50, 10 when left out) and `skip` (0 or more) from the query. Answers with `data` (the deliveries, newest first), `hasMore` and `nextSkip`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The webhook. |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `limit` | number |  | How many rows to return, from 1 to 50. 10 when left out. |
| `skip` | number |  | How many rows to pass over. 0 when left out. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/webhooks/<id>/logs?limit=10&skip=0
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Delivery log fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<log id>",
      "webhookId": "<webhook id>",
      "event": "task.updated",
      "statusCode": 200,
      "success": true,
      "durationMs": 180,
      "attempt": 1,
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ],
  "hasMore": true,
  "nextSkip": 10
}
```

**Paging**

Skip and limit. `hasMore` and `nextSkip` sit beside `data`; send `nextSkip` back as `skip` while `hasMore` is `true`.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The id is not an id. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The webhook does not exist, or is someone else's. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Goals

Goals with targets that show how far a team or a person has got. A goal has an owner, a period and up to 20 targets; its progress is the weighted average of its targets. A target is a `number`, a `currency` amount, a `boolean` or a `tasks` target counted from lists and tasks. A goal is `private` (its owner), `people` (its owner and the people it is shared with) or `workspace` (every member; a guest only when named). Guests can read goals shared with them and cannot create one. A token limited to projects cannot use these routes. A write made with an agent's token follows the rules that hold that agent.

### List goals

`GET /api/v2/goals` · beta

Lists the goals the caller can read, by name.

A counted target is answered with the numbers last counted; a count that is due is made again behind the answer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. Only goals the caller can read are listed |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `archived` | boolean |  | `true` lists the archived goals instead of the live ones. |
| `mine` | boolean |  | `true` keeps the goals the caller owns or is named on. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/goals
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Goals fetched successfully.",
  "data": [
    {
      "_id": "<goal id>",
      "name": "Launch 2.0",
      "description": "",
      "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
      "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
      "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
      "visibility": "workspace",
      "sharedWith": [],
      "sharedWithMe": false,
      "isOwner": true,
      "color": "#2F3990",
      "progressPct": 40,
      "targets": [
        {
          "id": "<target id>",
          "name": "Revenue",
          "kind": "currency",
          "weight": 1,
          "progressPct": 40,
          "reachedAt": null,
          "start": 0,
          "target": 50000,
          "current": 20000,
          "unit": "",
          "currencyCode": "USD",
          "updatedBy": "<user id>",
          "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
        }
      ],
      "archived": false,
      "canEdit": true,
      "canSetValue": true,
      "createdBy": "<user id>",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Paging**

None. The whole list is returned.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `archived` or `mine` is not `true` or `false`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Create a goal

`POST /api/v2/goals` · beta

Creates a goal, with its targets if they are sent.

The caller becomes its owner. A goal starts `private` unless `visibility` says otherwise. A `tasks` target is counted before the answer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace that is not a guest |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | The goal's name, 1 to 120 characters. |
| `description` | string |  | Up to 2000 characters. |
| `periodStart` | date |  | The first day, written `YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty. |
| `periodEnd` | date |  | The last day, written `YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty. Not before `periodStart`. |
| `visibility` | string |  | `private`, `people` or `workspace`. Defaults to `private`. |
| `sharedWith` | id[] |  | Up to 100 active members to share the goal with. It must be empty for a `private` goal. |
| `color` | string |  | A colour written `#rrggbb`, or empty. |
| `targets` | object[] |  | Up to 20 targets, each as described under [Add a target](#add-a-target) without the goal. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/goals
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Launch 2.0",
  "visibility": "workspace",
  "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
  "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
  "targets": [
    {
      "name": "Revenue",
      "kind": "currency",
      "currencyCode": "USD",
      "start": 0,
      "target": 50000
    }
  ]
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Goal saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a value of the wrong type or size, or a body that changes nothing. The answer names the `field` that was refused. A person can own at most 200 goals. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List the goals a task counts towards

`GET /api/v2/goals/for-task/:taskId` · beta

Lists the targets that count a task, with the goal each belongs to.

A target counts a task when it names the task, or names its list and the task is a top-level task. Only live goals the caller can read are listed.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The task must be one the caller can open |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `taskId` | id | The task. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/goals/for-task/<taskId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Goals fetched successfully.",
  "data": [
    {
      "goalId": "<goal id>",
      "goalName": "Launch 2.0",
      "color": "#2F3990",
      "progressPct": 40,
      "targetId": "<target id>",
      "targetName": "Ship the tasks",
      "targetProgressPct": 60,
      "through": "task"
    }
  ]
}
```

`through` is `task` when the target names the task and `list` when it counts the task's list. The rows are ordered by goal name, then target name.

**Paging**

None.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The task does not exist, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Read a goal

`GET /api/v2/goals/:id` · beta

Returns one goal, live or archived.

Needs an active seat in the workspace; a token limited to some projects is refused with 403. A goal is readable by its owner, by people it is shared with by name, and, when its visibility is `workspace`, by every member except guests, who need to be named. A goal the caller cannot read answers 404 as one that does not exist does. Answers with `data`: the goal, live or archived, with its targets, `progressPct`, and `canEdit`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/goals/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Goal fetched successfully.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a goal

`PATCH /api/v2/goals/:id` · beta

Changes a goal's name, period, audience, colour or owner. Only the keys sent are changed.

Targets have routes of their own. Changing `visibility`, `sharedWith` or `ownerUserId` is refused with the code `sources_would_drop`, and the `sources` that would drop out, when the goal counts tasks that not everyone who would then read it can open. Making a goal `private` empties `sharedWith`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | 1 to 120 characters. |
| `description` | string |  | Up to 2000 characters. |
| `periodStart` | date |  | Written `YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty. |
| `periodEnd` | date |  | Written `YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty. Not before `periodStart`. |
| `visibility` | string |  | `private`, `people` or `workspace`. |
| `sharedWith` | id[] |  | Up to 100 active members. It must be empty for a `private` goal. |
| `color` | string |  | A colour written `#rrggbb`, or empty. |
| `ownerUserId` | id |  | The new owner: an active member who is not a guest. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/goals/<id>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "visibility": "people", "sharedWith": ["<user id>"] }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Goal saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a value of the wrong type or size, or a body that changes nothing. The answer names the `field` that was refused. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 409 | The goal is archived. Restore it first. The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Summarise a goal

`POST /api/v2/goals/:id/summary` · beta

Writes a short summary of a goal's progress with the workspace's AI, keeps it on the goal and answers with the whole goal.

The summary is made only when this route is called, from the goal's name, its period and its targets with their numbers. It is kept until the next one is made; `stale` says the numbers have changed since.

It needs AI switched on for the workspace and a model the server can reach, and it is booked to the workspace's AI budget.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |

The body must be empty.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/goals/<id>/summary
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Summary saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "summary": {
      "text": "Launch 2.0 is 40% of the way. Revenue stands at 20,000 of 50,000 USD with the period ending on 31 December.",
      "madeAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "stale": false
    }
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as in [Read a goal](#read-a-goal), with its new `summary`.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is not empty. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 402 | The workspace's AI budget is spent. The answer has `code: "ai_budget_exhausted"`. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace, or can read the goal and may not change it. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 409 | The goal is archived, or AI is switched off or not set up for the workspace (`code: "ai_unavailable"`). |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |
| 502 | The model gave no usable answer (`code: "summary_failed"`). Send the request again. |

### Archive a goal

`POST /api/v2/goals/:id/archive` · beta

Archives a goal. It leaves the live list and can be restored.

Allowed for the goal's owner, and for owners and admins when the goal's visibility is `workspace`; a goal the caller can read but not change answers 403. A token limited to some projects is refused. The body must be empty. Answers with `data`: the archived goal.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |

The body must be empty.

A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is not empty. |

| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 409 | The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |
