### Add a target

`POST /api/v2/goals/:id/targets` · beta

Adds a target to a goal and answers with the whole goal.

A goal holds at most 20 targets. A `tasks` target is counted before the answer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | The target's name, 1 to 120 characters. |
| `kind` | string | yes | `number`, `currency`, `boolean` or `tasks`. It cannot be changed afterwards. |
| `weight` | integer |  | How much the target counts towards the goal, 1 to 100. Defaults to 1. |
| `start` | number |  | For `number` and `currency`: where the range starts. Defaults to 0. |
| `target` | number |  | For `number` and `currency`: where the range ends. Required, and different from `start`. |
| `current` | number |  | For `number` and `currency`: the value now. Defaults to `start`. |
| `unit` | string |  | For `number` and `currency`: a label, up to 20 characters. |
| `currencyCode` | string |  | For `currency`: a three-letter code of a currency the workspace uses. Defaults to the workspace's own currency. |
| `done` | boolean |  | For `boolean`: whether it is done. Defaults to `false`. |
| `sources` | object |  | For a `tasks` target: `{ sprintIds, taskIds }`, at most 20 lists and 100 tasks. Each must be one the caller can open and every reader of the goal can open. |

Only the keys of the target's `kind` are accepted; any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/goals/<id>/targets
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Revenue",
  "kind": "currency",
  "currencyCode": "USD",
  "start": 0,
  "target": 50000
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Target added.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a value of the wrong type or size, or a body that changes nothing. The answer names the `field` that was refused. The goal already holds 20 targets, a currency is not one of the workspace's, or a `tasks` source is not one the caller can open and every reader of the goal can open (the code is `source_not_found` or `source_not_shared`). |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. |
| 409 | The goal is archived. Restore it first. The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Change a target

`PATCH /api/v2/goals/:id/targets/:targetId` · beta

Changes what a target is: its name, weight, range, unit, currency or sources. Its value has a route of its own.

The target's `kind` cannot change. Changing the `sources` of a `tasks` target counts it again before the answer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |
| `targetId` | id | The target. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | The target's name, 1 to 120 characters. |
| `weight` | integer |  | How much the target counts towards the goal, 1 to 100. |
| `start` | number |  | For `number` and `currency`: where the range starts. |
| `target` | number |  | For `number` and `currency`: where the range ends. Different from `start`. |
| `unit` | string |  | For `number` and `currency`: a label, up to 20 characters. |
| `currencyCode` | string |  | For `currency`: a three-letter code of a currency the workspace uses. |
| `sources` | object |  | For a `tasks` target: `{ sprintIds, taskIds }`, at most 20 lists and 100 tasks. Each must be one the caller can open and every reader of the goal can open. |

Only the keys of the target's `kind` are accepted, without its value; any other key is refused. The body must name something to change.

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/goals/<id>/targets/<targetId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "target": 60000 }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Target saved.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a value of the wrong type or size, or a body that changes nothing. The answer names the `field` that was refused. A `tasks` source is not one the caller can open and every reader of the goal can open. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. Or the goal has no such target. |
| 409 | The goal is archived. Restore it first. The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Remove a target

`DELETE /api/v2/goals/:id/targets/:targetId` · beta

Removes a target and answers with the whole goal.

Allowed for the goal's owner, and for owners and admins when the goal's visibility is `workspace`; a goal the caller can read but not change answers 403, and an archived goal answers 409. The body must be empty and an unknown `targetId` answers 404. Answers with `data`: the whole goal without that target.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |
| `targetId` | id | The target. |

The body must be empty.

**Request**

```http
DELETE /api/v2/goals/<id>/targets/<targetId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Target removed.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 0,
    "targets": [],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body is not empty. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. Or the goal has no such target. |
| 409 | The goal is archived. Restore it first. The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Set a target's value

`PUT /api/v2/goals/:id/targets/:targetId/value` · beta

Sets the current value of a `number` or `currency` target, or whether a `boolean` target is done.

The goal's progress is worked out again. A `tasks` target is counted from its tasks and cannot be set by hand: the answer is a refusal with the code `counted_from_tasks`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | An active seat in the workspace. The goal must be one the caller can read, and one the caller can change: its owner, or an owner or admin when the goal is visible to the workspace |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The goal. |
| `targetId` | id | The target. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `current` | number |  | For a `number` or `currency` target: the value now. |
| `done` | boolean |  | For a `boolean` target: whether it is done. |

Send the one key the target's `kind` takes; any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
PUT /api/v2/goals/<id>/targets/<targetId>/value
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "current": 25000 }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Target updated.",
  "data": {
    "_id": "<goal id>",
    "name": "Launch 2.0",
    "description": "",
    "ownerUserId": "<user id>",
    "periodStart": "2026-10-01",
    "periodEnd": "2026-12-31",
    "visibility": "workspace",
    "sharedWith": [],
    "sharedWithMe": false,
    "isOwner": true,
    "color": "#2F3990",
    "progressPct": 40,
    "targets": [
      {
        "id": "<target id>",
        "name": "Revenue",
        "kind": "currency",
        "weight": 1,
        "progressPct": 40,
        "reachedAt": null,
        "start": 0,
        "target": 50000,
        "current": 20000,
        "unit": "",
        "currencyCode": "USD",
        "updatedBy": "<user id>",
        "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
      }
    ],
    "archived": false,
    "canEdit": true,
    "canSetValue": true,
    "createdBy": "<user id>",
    "createdAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

`data` is the goal as the caller may see it; the example shows one currency target. A `boolean` target carries `done`; a `tasks` target carries `sources`, `counted`, `notCounted`, `dirty`, `updating` and `sourceNames` in place of `start`, `target` and `current`. `sharedWith` lists people only for someone who can change the goal. `canEdit` and `canSetValue` say what the caller may do with it. A goal that has been summarised carries `summary`: its `text`, when it was made (`madeAt`) and whether the numbers have changed since (`stale`).

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a value of the wrong type or size, or a body that changes nothing. The answer names the `field` that was refused. The key the target needs is missing, or the target is counted from tasks. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller has no active seat in the workspace. |
| 404 | The goal does not exist, or the caller cannot read it. Or the goal has no such target. |
| 409 | The goal is archived. Restore it first. The goal was changed at the same moment. Send the request again. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Whiteboards

One whiteboard for each list: task cards, sticky notes and text laid out on a canvas. A board is as open as its list: it can be read by whoever can open the list, and changed by whoever can edit the project while the list, its folders and the project are active. The title of a task card is shown only to someone who may open that task.

### Read a whiteboard

`GET /api/v2/whiteboards/:projectId/:sprintId` · beta

Returns the board of a list: its cards, notes and texts.

A list that has no board yet answers with an empty one at revision 0.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to open the list |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id | The project. |
| `sprintId` | id | The list. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/whiteboards/<projectId>/<sprintId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Whiteboard fetched.",
  "data": {
    "boardId": "<board id>",
    "revision": 3,
    "elements": [
      {
        "id": "card1",
        "type": "task",
        "x": 120,
        "y": 80,
        "z": 0,
        "taskId": "<task id>",
        "title": "Write the release notes",
        "taskKey": "WEB-12"
      },
      {
        "id": "note1",
        "type": "note",
        "text": "Ask design",
        "tone": "amber",
        "x": 400,
        "y": 80,
        "w": 200,
        "h": 120,
        "z": 1
      }
    ],
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "canEdit": true,
    "limits": { "elements": 2000, "notes": 500, "text": 2000 }
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `elements` | A `task` card (`id`, `type`, `x`, `y`, `z`, `taskId`, `title`, `taskKey`), a `note` (`text`, `tone`, `x`, `y`, `w`, `h`, `z`) or a `text` (the same without `tone`). A card for a task the caller may not open carries only its place and `withheld: true`. A card whose task was deleted or moved to another list is left out. |
| `revision` | The number a save names as `baseRevision`. |
| `canEdit` | Whether the caller may change the board now. |
| `limits` | The most cards, notes and texts a board holds, and the longest text. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The project or list does not exist, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Save a whiteboard

`PATCH /api/v2/whiteboards/:projectId/:sprintId` · beta

Adds, moves and removes elements on a board, as a change on the revision the caller last read.

A card names the task it stands for and a task has one card: a second card for it moves the first. A note or a text is replaced whole. A new card is left out, with no word of why, when its task is not a live task of the list or one the caller may not open. A save that changes nothing is answered without a new revision.

The state a save replaces is kept in the history: when someone else is about to change the board, when the save touches half the board or more, and otherwise at most once every ten minutes. The history holds the last 20 states.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the project, and the list, its folders and the project must be active |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id | The project. |
| `sprintId` | id | The list. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `baseRevision` | integer | yes | The `revision` the change was made on. |
| `upsert` | object[] |  | The elements to add or replace, at most 2500 of them, each named once. A `task` card carries `id`, `type`, `taskId`, `x`, `y` and `z`. A `note` carries `id`, `type`, `text`, `tone`, `x`, `y`, `w`, `h` and `z`; a `text` the same without `tone`. |
| `remove` | string[] |  | The ids of the elements to take off. |

Name at least one of `upsert` and `remove`. An element id is up to 40 letters, digits, `-` and `_`. `x` and `y` are numbers from 0 to 100000, `w` from 40 to 1200, `h` from 24 to 1200, `z` a whole number up to 1000000 (default 0), `text` up to 2000 characters, `tone` one of `amber`, `green`, `red`, `violet`, `brand` or `grey`. A board holds at most 2000 cards and 500 notes and texts. One save is at most 512 KB. Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
PATCH /api/v2/whiteboards/<projectId>/<sprintId>
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "baseRevision": 3,
  "upsert": [
    {
      "id": "note1",
      "type": "note",
      "text": "Ask design",
      "tone": "amber",
      "x": 400,
      "y": 80,
      "w": 200,
      "h": 120
    }
  ],
  "remove": ["card2"]
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Whiteboard saved.",
  "data": {
    "boardId": "<board id>",
    "revision": 4,
    "elements": [],
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "canEdit": true,
    "limits": { "elements": 2000, "notes": 500, "text": 2000 }
  }
}
```

`data` is the board after the save, as in [Read a whiteboard](#read-a-whiteboard); the example leaves `elements` empty.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | A key that is not known, a field of the wrong type or size, or a save that changes nothing. The answer names the `field`. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 409 | The board changed since `baseRevision`. The answer has `code: "revision_conflict"` and the current board in `data`. |
| 413 | The save, or the board it would make, is too large. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### List a whiteboard's history

`GET /api/v2/whiteboards/:projectId/:sprintId/history` · beta

Lists the earlier states kept for a board, newest first.

Needs a project and list the caller can open; anything else answers 404 `Whiteboard not found.`. Answers with `data`: the earlier states kept for the board, newest first, each with `revision`, `savedBy`, `savedAt`, `cards` (how many elements) and `reason`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `read` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to open the list |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id | The project. |
| `sprintId` | id | The list. |

**Request**

```http
GET /api/v2/whiteboards/<projectId>/<sprintId>/history
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Whiteboard history fetched.",
  "data": [
    {
      "revision": 3,
      "savedBy": "<user id>",
      "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
      "cards": 12,
      "reason": "interval"
    }
  ]
}
```

`revision` is the state to name in a restore. `cards` counts every element of that state. `reason` says why it was kept: `author` (someone else was about to change the board), `bulk` (a save touched half the board or more), `interval` (ten minutes had passed) or `restore` (the state before a restore).

**Paging**

None. A board keeps at most 20 states.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `read` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The project or list does not exist, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Restore a whiteboard

`POST /api/v2/whiteboards/:projectId/:sprintId/restore` · beta

Puts an earlier state of a board back, as a new revision.

The state being replaced is kept in the history. Cards whose tasks are gone are left out.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to edit the project, and the list, its folders and the project must be active |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `projectId` | id | The project. |
| `sprintId` | id | The list. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `revision` | integer | yes | The `revision` of a state in the history, 1 or more. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/whiteboards/<projectId>/<sprintId>/restore
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "revision": 3 }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "Whiteboard restored.",
  "data": {
    "boardId": "<board id>",
    "revision": 5,
    "elements": [],
    "savedBy": "<user id>",
    "savedAt": "2026-10-01T09:30:00.000Z",
    "canEdit": true,
    "limits": { "elements": 2000, "notes": 500, "text": 2000 }
  }
}
```

`data` is the board after the restore, as in [Read a whiteboard](#read-a-whiteboard); the example leaves `elements` empty.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `revision` is not a whole number of 1 or more. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The project or list does not exist, or the caller cannot open it; or that earlier state is no longer kept. |
| 409 | The board changed while it was being restored. The answer has `code: "revision_conflict"` and the current board in `data`. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Imports

Undoing an import that created tasks in bulk.

### Undo an import

`POST /api/v2/imports/:id/undo` · beta

Moves the tasks an import created to the trash.

The custom fields the import created are switched off when no task outside the trash still holds a value in them. A field that has since been made a field of every project stays, and so do statuses and tags. A task the import only updated is left alone. Trashing a task takes the tasks under it, so a task that is kept keeps every task above it.

An import with tasks that someone has worked on since (a history line other than its creation, a comment the import did not bring, or a subtask it did not make) is not undone unless `keepEdited` is `true`; those tasks are then left where they are.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The person who ran the import, an owner or an admin |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | id | The import, from `GET /api/v2/imports`. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `keepEdited` | boolean |  | `true` to undo the import and keep the tasks that have been worked on. Defaults to `false`. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/imports/<id>/undo
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "keepEdited": true }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "38 imported task(s) moved to the trash.",
  "data": {
    "trashed": 38,
    "kept": [{ "id": "<task id>", "name": "Plan the launch" }],
    "keptCount": 1,
    "fieldsRemoved": ["Story points"],
    "fieldsKept": []
  }
}
```

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `trashed` | How many tasks were moved to the trash. |
| `kept` | The tasks left where they are, at most 50, each as `{ id, name }`. |
| `keptCount` | How many tasks were kept. |
| `fieldsRemoved` | The titles of the custom fields that were switched off. |
| `fieldsKept` | The titles of the custom fields the import created that are still in use. |

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 404 | The import does not exist. |
| 409 | The import was already undone (`code: "ALREADY_UNDONE"`); nothing it created is left (`NOTHING_TO_UNDO`); or tasks were worked on since and `keepEdited` was not sent (`EDITED`, with `data.edited`, the first 50 of them as `{ id, name }`, and `data.editedCount`). |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Custom fields

Reading the tasks a relationship field links and the votes on a voting field, and casting a vote.

### Resolve relationship and voting values

`POST /api/v2/custom-fields/links/resolve` · beta

Returns, for each task named, the tasks its relationship fields link and the tally of its voting fields.

A task the caller cannot open is left out as if it held nothing. A linked task the caller cannot open is left out of its list. The voters of a field are named only when the field shows them. A task with no values is not in the answer.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | Only tasks the caller can open are answered |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `taskIds` | id[] | yes | The tasks, at most 200. |

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/custom-fields/links/resolve
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "taskIds": ["<task id>"] }
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "statusText": "OK",
  "data": {
    "<task id>": {
      "<relationship field id>": [
        {
          "id": "<task id>",
          "key": "WEB-12",
          "title": "Write the release notes",
          "status": { "key": 1, "text": "Open", "type": "default_active" },
          "projectId": "<project id>",
          "sprintId": "<list id>",
          "folderId": ""
        }
      ],
      "<voting field id>": { "count": 4, "voted": true }
    }
  }
}
```

A relationship field answers with its linked tasks. A voting field answers with `count`, `voted` (whether the caller has voted) and, when the field shows voters, `voters`, a list of user ids.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | `taskIds` is not a list, or lists more than 200 tasks. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

### Vote on a task

`POST /api/v2/custom-fields/:fieldId/vote` · beta

Casts or withdraws the caller's own vote on a voting field of a task.

The voter is always the caller.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Auth | Session or API token with the `write` scope |
| Token limited to projects | Refused |
| Permission | The caller must be able to open the task and to read its custom fields |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `fieldId` | id | The voting field. |

**Body**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `taskId` | id | yes | The task. |
| `vote` | boolean | yes | `true` to cast the vote, `false` to withdraw it. |

Any other key is refused.

**Request**

```http
POST /api/v2/custom-fields/<fieldId>/vote
Authorization: Bearer <token>
companyid: <company id>
Content-Type: application/json

{ "taskId": "<task id>", "vote": true }
```

**Response**

```json
{ "status": true, "statusText": "OK", "data": { "count": 5, "voted": true } }
```

`count` is the votes on the field after the change.

**Errors**

| Status | When |
|---|---|
| 400 | The body names more than `taskId` and `vote`, `vote` is not `true` or `false`, or the field is not a voting field of that task. |
| 401 | No token, a token that is invalid, expired or revoked, or no valid `companyid` header. |
| 403 | The token lacks the `write` scope, or its person is no longer a member of the workspace. A token limited to projects is refused here. The caller's role gives no access to the custom fields of the task. |
| 404 | The task does not exist, or the caller cannot open it. |
| 429 | Too many requests from this address. Wait `retryAfter` seconds and send it again. |

## Appendix: every other route

Every route the server registers that is not documented in full above, with every optional module switched on. `internal` routes are not for integrations; every `/api/v1` route is one unless the meta file says otherwise. `undocumented` routes have no description here yet; nothing about them is promised.

The Auth column is the guard the route sits behind. `Session or token` routes accept the web app's session or an API token. `Session` routes refuse a token. `Instance admin` routes are for the person who runs the server. `No login guard` routes are sign-in and sign-up, pages opened by a secret link, and endpoints that check a credential of their own, such as `/mcp` and `/scim/v2`. A handler can refuse more than its guard does.

| `PUT` | `/api/v2/ai-switch` | Session | internal | Turns AI on or off for the workspace. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/api-tokens` | Session | internal | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it lists only that person's own tokens. Answers with `data` (each token's name, prefix, scopes, state and dates, never the secret) and `policy`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/api-tokens` | Session | internal | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, for the caller's own token. Reads `name` (string), `scopes` (list from the scopes the server offers) and `expiresInDays` (number) from the body. Answers with `data`: the token with its secret, shown once. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/:id` | Session | internal | Only the person who owns the token. Reads `name` (string) and `active` (boolean) from the body and changes the ones sent; a body with neither answers `Nothing to update.`. Answers with `data`: the token. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/:id` | Session | internal | Only the person who owns the token; any other id answers `Token not found.`. Removes the token and its activity log. Answers with `statusText` `Token deleted.` and no `data`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/:id/logs` | Session | internal | Only the person who owns the token; any other id answers `Token not found.`. Answers with `data`: the latest 50 calls made with the token, newest first. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/:id/renew` | Session | internal | Only the person who owns the token, and not with an API token (403). A token that was revoked cannot be renewed. Answers with `data`: the token with a new secret, shown once, and a new end date. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/ai-connection` | Session | internal | Whether the signed-in person's own AI app has connected to this workspace, when it last called, and which groups of tools this server offers it. Never a token or an app's name. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/mcp` | Session | internal | Any signed-in member of the workspace, and not with an API token (403). Reads `name` (string), `mode` and `provider` (strings), `projectIds` (id list), `expiresInDays` (number), `label` (string), `scopes` and `grants` (lists) from the body; the account `mode` must be one the workspace allows. Answers with `data`: the token, shown once, and the address of the MCP server. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/needing-expiry` | Session | internal | Owners and admins only, and not with an API token (403). Answers with `data`: tokens in the workspace that have no expiry date, and the token `policy`; an empty list while the strict token policy is off. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/api-tokens/step-credentials` | Session | internal | Any member of the workspace, and not with an API token (403). Answers with `data`: the live step credentials, owners and admins seeing all of them and others the ones they started, and `policy`; an empty list while step credentials are off. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/project/:projectId` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open. Answers with `data`: `rules` (`entries` of person and sentence, `fallbackUserId`, `mode`, `onCreate`, `onChange`) and `ai.state`, whether AI is available. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/project/:projectId` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change the project's details. Reads `entries` (a list of at most 50 objects with `userId` and `when`, a sentence of at most 300 characters, one per person), `fallbackUserId` (id or empty), `mode` (`suggest` or `apply`, `suggest` when left out), `onCreate` (boolean, true when left out) and `onChange` (boolean, false when left out) from the body; each person must hold a seat and be able to open the project. Answers with `data`: the saved rules. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/project/:projectId/draft` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change the project's details, and AI must be on with a model configured (409 otherwise). Reads `userIds` (a list of 1 to 10 ids) from the body. Answers with `data.drafts`: a suggested rule sentence for each person who can open the project, based on their recent tasks there. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/task/:taskId` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a task the caller can open (404 otherwise). Answers with `data`: `decision`, the latest open suggestion for the task or `null`, and `pending`, whether one is still being worked out. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/task/:taskId/decisions/:decisionId/accept` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change a task's assignee in the task's project. Assigns the suggested person to the task; 409 when the suggestion was already handled, the task already has an assignee, or the person can no longer open it. Answers with `data`: the decision. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/task/:taskId/decisions/:decisionId/dismiss` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change a task's assignee in the task's project. Marks the suggestion dismissed. Answers with `data`: the decision. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/assignment-rules/task/:taskId/decisions/:decisionId/undo` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change a task's assignee in the task's project. Takes back an accepted suggestion. Answers with `data`: the decision. |

| `POST` | `/api/v2/automations/draft` | Session or token | undocumented | Drafts an automation from a sentence. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/automations/registry` | Session or token | undocumented | The triggers and actions an automation can use. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/billing/client-view` | Session or token | undocumented | What a project's client sees of its billing. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/billing/client-view/message` | Session or token | undocumented | Sends a message from the client view. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/billing/contract` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads a project's billing contract. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/billing/contract` | Session or token | undocumented | Changes a project's billing contract. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/billing/hourly` | Session or token | undocumented | A month's billable hours for a project. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/billing/milestone` | Session or token | undocumented | Adds a billing milestone. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v2/billing/milestone/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Changes a billing milestone. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/calls/ice-config` | Session or token | internal | The connection settings for a call. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/calls/notes` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists the caller's own call notes. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/calls/notes` | Session or token | undocumented | Saves the notes of a call. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/calls/notes/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads one set of call notes. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v2/calls/notes/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Changes a set of call notes. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/changelog` | No login guard | internal | The product changelog shown in the web app. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/checkPermission` | No login guard | internal | Checks an invitation link. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/company/create` | Session or token | internal | Creates a workspace. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/company/delete` | Session | internal | Deletes a workspace. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/:connector` | Session | internal | Ends the signed-in member's Google connection: revokes the grant with Google and clears the stored tokens. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/:connector/complete` | Session | internal | Completes the connection the same session started: exchanges the code with the PKCE verifier and keeps the tokens in the secrets store. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/:connector/connect` | Session | internal | Starts the signed-in member's Google connection and answers Google's consent address. The state is single-use, lasts ten minutes and belongs to this session. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/:connector/members/:userId` | Session | internal | For an owner or admin: ends a member's Google connection. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/members` | Session | internal | For an owner or admin: which members hold a Google connection, with its scopes, dates and state. Never the account or a token. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/connectors/google/mine` | Session | internal | Shows the signed-in member's own Google connections: the account's email, the granted scopes, the dates and the state. Never a token or its handle. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/connectors/slack` | Session | internal | Shows the workspace's Slack connector: whether each secret is set and since when, the channel list and, for each allowed channel, whether agents may read it or post to it. Never a secret. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/connectors/slack/channels` | Session | internal | Sets, for each allowed Slack channel, whether agents may read it, propose a message to it, or both. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/connectors/slack/channels/refresh` | Session | internal | Reads the Slack channel list again. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/connectors/slack/secrets` | Session | internal | Stores the Slack bot token or signing secret in the secrets store, after checking the token with Slack. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/connectors/slack/secrets/:key` | Session | internal | Removes a Slack connector secret. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/createUser` | No login guard | internal | Signs up. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/csp-report` | No login guard | internal | Receives a browser's content security policy report. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/:fieldId/ai/apply` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace who can edit that field on the task. Reads `proposalIds` (a list of up to 5 ids from a preview the same person made) from the body; an expired one is refused. Answers with `data`: `applied` (task ids) and `refused` (with a reason each). |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/:fieldId/ai/jobs` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace who can edit that field on the tasks. Reads `taskIds` (a list of ids, up to the workspace's bulk limit) and `proposalIds` (a list of up to 5 ids) from the body. The fill runs in the background. Answers with `data`: the job. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/:fieldId/ai/preview` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace who can edit that field on the task; a task they cannot edit it on comes back with a `reason` instead. Reads `taskIds` (a list of 1 to 5 ids) from the body. Nothing is written. Answers with `data.proposals`: for each task, a `proposalId`, the proposed `text` and `fieldValue`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/ai/jobs/:jobId` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace, and only for a job that person started; any other id answers 404 `Job not found.`. Answers with `data`: the job and its progress. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/compute` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change a task's custom field. Reads `taskIds` (a list of 1 to 200 ids) and `scope` (`sprint` or left out) from the body; only tasks the caller can open are computed. Answers with `data`: `updated`, the computed `values` and `errors`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/formula/scope` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `projectId` (id) and `taskId` (id, used only when the caller can open the task) from the query. Answers with `data`: `names` a formula may use, with the task's values when a task is named, and the lists of `functions` and `rollupFunctions`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/custom-fields/formula/validate` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `expression` (string, required), `fieldId`, `fieldTitle`, `projectId` (ids and string) and `sample` (an object of values to try it on) from the body. A formula that does not parse or that closes a cycle answers a failed status with a `code`. Answers with `data`: `references`, `functions`, `preview` and `previewError`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/deleteManualLogtime` | Session or token | undocumented | Deletes a manual time entry. Needs a task the caller can open. Reads `timeSheetId`, `logTimeDate`, `dateFormat`, `startLogTime`, `endLogTime`, `projectId`, `ticketId`, `sprintId`, `taskName` and `projectName` from the body, all required, plus `timeDuration` (minutes), `userId` and `folderId`. Deleting another person's entry needs the Everyone grant on the user timesheet and an open project, else 403. An entry in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText` `Logtime deleted successfully`. It also writes task history and a notification. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/epics` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open, named by the query field `projectId` (id). Answers with `data`: the project's epics, oldest first, with `taskCount` and `completedCount` counted over the tasks the caller can open. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/epics` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the project named by `projectId`. Reads `name` (string, required), `projectId` (id, required), `description`, `color`, `priority`, `ownerUserId` (strings), `startDate` and `dueDate` from the body; a failed check answers a failed status with the reason. Answers with `data`: the new epic. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/epics/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the epic's project. Reads `name`, `color`, `description`, `status`, `priority`, `ownerUserId`, `startDate` and `dueDate` from the body and changes the ones sent; a body with none of them answers a failed status. Answers with `data`: the epic as it is now. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/epics/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the epic's project. The epic is marked deleted and its tasks are unlinked from it. Answers with `statusText` `Epic deleted.` and no `data`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/epics/:id/recount` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the epic's project. Counts the epic's tasks again and answers with `data`: the epic with `taskCount` and `completedCount`, counted for tasks the caller can open. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/epics/assign` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the projects of both the task and the epic. Reads `taskId` (id) and `epicId` (id, or empty or null to take the task out) from the body; a task the caller cannot open, or an epic that does not exist, answers a failed status. Answers with `data`: `taskId` and `epicId`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/exports` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it lists only the caller's own exports (a `uid` of someone else answers 403). Answers with `data`: up to 20 exports, newest first, each with `type`, `format`, `status`, `fileName`, `total`, `processed`, `error` and `createdAt`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/exports` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace who can list the project's tasks (404 otherwise). Reads `format` (`csv` or `xlsx`), `projectId` (id), `sprintId` (id, optional) and `projectName` (string) from the body. The export runs in the background. Answers with `data`: the job. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/exports/:id/download` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, for an export the caller started; a workspace export also needs the owner or admin role. Answers with the file once the export has finished, and 404 `Export not ready.` before that. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/exports/workspace` | Session or token | undocumented | Owners and admins only. Reads `format` (`csv` or `xlsx`, `xlsx` when left out) from the body. The export runs in the background. Answers with `data`: the job's `_id`, `type`, `format`, `status`, `fileName` and `createdAt`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/forms` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open, named by the query field `projectId` (id); otherwise 404. Answers with `data`: the project's forms, newest first, each with `title`, `description`, `sprintId`, `state` and `submissionCount`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/forms` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the project named in the body (403 without them, 404 when it cannot be seen). Reads `title` (string, required), `projectId` (id, required), `description` (string) and `sprintId` (id) from the body. The form starts as a draft with no questions. Answers with `data`: the form. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/forms/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open; otherwise 404 `Form not found.`. Answers with `data`: the form with its questions, and its public `url` while it is live. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/forms/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the form's project. Reads `title`, `description`, `successMessage` (strings), `sprintId` (id), `questions` (list), `defaults` (object) and `state` from the body and changes the ones sent; a failed check answers a failed status with the reason. Answers with `data`: the form as it is now. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/forms/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the form's project. A form that is published answers a failed status, and must be taken offline first. The form is marked deleted and the tasks it filed are kept. Answers with `statusText` `Form deleted.` and no `data`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/forms/:id/publish` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the form's project (403 without them, 404 when the project cannot be seen). Reads `publish` (boolean, true when left out; false takes the form offline) from the body. A form that fails the checks for going live answers a failed status with the reason. Answers with `data`: `state` and, when live, `token` and `url`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/forms/:id/submissions` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the form's project, because submissions hold the submitters' answers. Reads `q` (string, up to 120 characters, matched against every answer), `page` and `limit` (numbers, 10 per page by default and at most 100) and `all` (`1` returns up to 5000 rows for an export) from the query. Answers with `data`: `submissions`, `total`, `page`, `pages` and `truncated`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/forms/fields` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data`: the questions a form can ask, as `groups`, the `widgets`, `spans`, `gridColumns` and the task fields a question can be mapped to. |

| `GET` | `/api/v2/invoices/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads an invoice. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/invoices/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Changes a draft invoice. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/invoices/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Deletes a draft invoice. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/invoices/:id/paid` | Session or token | undocumented | Marks an invoice as paid. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/invoices/:id/send` | Session or token | undocumented | Issues an invoice. |
