| `POST` | `/api/v2/manualLogtime` | Session or token | undocumented | Adds a manual time entry to a task, or edits one. Needs a task the caller can open. Reads `logTimeDate`, `description`, `startLogTime`, `endLogTime`, `timeDuration` (a string like `HH:MM`), `ticketId`, `projectId`, `isEdit` (a boolean), `dateFormat`, `taskName`, `projectName`, `sprintId` and `timeZone` from the body, all required, plus `timeSheetId` and `previousLoggedTime` when `isEdit` is true. Optional: `userId`, `billable`, `folderId`, `companyOwnerId`. Logging for another person needs the Everyone grant on the user timesheet and the project must be open to the caller, else 403. The person must be a member (400). An entry in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText` `Logtime added successfully` and `data`: the saved entry. It also writes task history and a notification. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/oauth-client-approvals` | Session | internal | Lists OAuth clients waiting for approval. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/oauth-client-approvals/approve` | Session | internal | Approves an OAuth client. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/oauth-client-approvals/deny` | Session | internal | Denies an OAuth client. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/oauth-client-approvals/revoke` | Session | internal | Revokes an approval. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/oauth-clients` | Session | internal | Lists the workspace's OAuth clients. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/oauth-clients` | Session | internal | Registers an OAuth client. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/oauth-clients/:clientId` | Session | internal | Revokes an OAuth client. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/oauth-grants` | Session | internal | Lists the caller's own OAuth grants. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/oauth-grants/:grantId` | Session | internal | Revokes one of the caller's OAuth grants. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/oauth-grants/:grantId/withdraw` | Session | internal | Withdraws some of the permissions the caller gave a connected app. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/approve` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the page, and a share by name as editor is not enough; a person must make the call. Only a page an agent drafted can be approved, else a failed status. Answers with `data`: the page. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/comments` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member who may read the page. Answers with `data`: the page's comments, oldest first, up to 500, with `limit` and `atLimit`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/comments` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member who may read the page. Reads `message` (string), `blockId` (string), `parentId` (id of a comment to reply under) and an attached file as `mediaURL`, `mediaOriginalName` and `mediaSize` from the body. A page holds at most 500 comments (409). Answers with `data`: the comment. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/comments/:commentId` | Session or token | undocumented | Only the comment's author, who must be able to read the page (403 for anyone else). Reads `message` (string) from the body. Answers with `data`: the comment as it is now. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/comments/:commentId` | Session or token | undocumented | The comment's author or an admin, who must be able to read the page. A thread is deleted with its replies. Answers with `data._id`: the id of the comment. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/comments/:commentId/resolve` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member who may read the page; a thread that has an assignee can be resolved only by the assignee, the person who assigned it or an admin, and a reply cannot be resolved (400). Reads `resolved` (boolean, true when left out; false reopens the thread) from the body. Answers with `data`: the comment. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/images` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the page. Takes a multipart body with the image as `file`. Answers with `data.key`: the key to put in the page's image block. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/review` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs edit rights on the page, and a share by name as editor is not enough. Reads `nextReviewDate` (a date, three months ahead when left out) from the body, marks the page as a wiki page reviewed now by the caller, and answers with `data`: the page. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/shares` | Session or token | undocumented | Only the page's author, or an owner or admin who can open it (403 for anyone else). Answers with `data`: `people`, each with `userId`, `role`, `by`, `at` and `active`, and `limit`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/shares/:userId` | Session or token | undocumented | Only the page's author, or an owner or admin who can open it. Reads `role` (`viewer` or `editor`, `viewer` when left out) from the body; the person must be an active member of the workspace, and a page can be shared with a limited number of people (400 otherwise). Answers with `data`: the list of people the page is shared with. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/pages/:id/shares/:userId` | Session or token | undocumented | Only the page's author, or an owner or admin who can open it. Answers with `data`: the list of people the page is still shared with. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/pages/ai` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `action`, `title`, `instruction` and `currentText` (strings) and `pageId` (id of a page the caller may read, whose title and text fill in what is left out) from the body. Answers with `data`: the composed text, or a failed status with the reason, and `isNotAi` when no AI is set up. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/pages/ai-status` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data.configured`: whether an AI provider is set up for writing pages. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/prepare-notification-data` | Session or token | internal | Prepares notification data for the web app. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/provider-keys` | Session | internal | Lists the workspace's AI provider keys, by handle only. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/provider-keys/:provider` | Session | internal | Stores a workspace AI provider key. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/provider-keys/:provider` | Session | internal | Removes a workspace AI provider key. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/public-shares` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists the public links of something. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/public-shares` | Session or token | undocumented | Creates a public link to a list, a report or a page. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/public-shares/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Switches a public link on or off. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/public-shares/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Revokes a public link. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/reactions` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace who can read the target. Reads `targetType` (`task` or `comment`), `targetId` (id), `emoji` (string) and `isProjectComment` (boolean) from the body; the caller's own reaction is added, or removed if it is already there. A target the caller cannot read answers 404. Answers with `data.reactions`: the target's reactions. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/recent-visits` | Session or token | internal | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it lists that person's own visits. Reads `types` (a comma list of `task`, `project`, `sprint` and `doc`, or `all`; tasks only when left out) and `limit` (a number) from the query. Visits to anything deleted or no longer open to the caller drop out. Answers with `data`: the newest visits first. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/recent-visits` | Session or token | internal | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it records a visit for that person only. Reads `entityType` (`task`, `project`, `sprint` or `doc`) and `entityId` (id) from the body. Answers with `statusText` `Visit recorded.` and no `data`. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/sample-data` | Session or token | internal | Removes the sample data a new workspace starts with. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/scim/config` | Session or token | internal | Reads the workspace's SCIM settings. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/scim/config` | Session or token | internal | Changes the workspace's SCIM settings. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/scim/token` | Session or token | internal | Issues a new SCIM token for the workspace. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/search` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `query` (string) from the body and matches it against task names and keys, project names, comment text and page titles, within the projects, lists and pages the caller can open. Answers with `data`: `tasks`, `projects`, `comments` and `pages`, each limited in number. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/secrets` | Session | internal | Lists the workspace's secrets, by handle only. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/secrets` | Session | internal | Stores a workspace secret. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/secrets/:handle/revoke` | Session | internal | Revokes a workspace secret. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/secrets/:handle/rotate` | Session | internal | Replaces a workspace secret. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sendForgotPasswordEmail` | No login guard | internal | Emails a password reset link. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sendInvitationEmail` | Session or token | internal | Invites people to the workspace. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sendVerificationEmail` | No login guard | internal | Emails a verification link. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/session/delete` | Session or token | internal | Ends the person's sessions. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/session/delete/:id` | Session or token | internal | Ends one session. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/session/update` | Session or token | internal | Updates the current session's record. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/setup/complete` | No login guard | internal | Finishes the first-run setup. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/setup/events/:id` | No login guard | internal | The progress stream of the first-run setup. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/setup/status` | No login guard | internal | Whether the instance has been set up. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/backlog` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open. Reads `projectId` (id) from the body. Answers with `data`: the project's backlog list, which is made first if it does not exist. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/burndown` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open and a list the caller can see. Reads `sprintId` (id) from the body. Answers with `data`: the sprint's name, state, goal and dates, its totals of tasks, estimate and points, and a `days` series with the ideal line. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/complete` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs write access to the list's project and the permission to create lists there; a person must make the call. Reads `sprintId` (id) and `incompleteDestination` (`next`, `backlog` or the id of an open sprint of the same project, `next` when left out) from the body. Unfinished tasks move to the destination. Answers with `data`: the completed sprint; a sprint already completed answers success and moves nothing. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sprints/complete-preview` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open and a list the caller can see. Reads `sprintId` (id) from the query; a list that is not a sprint answers a failed status. Answers with `data`: the sprint's state, commitment, tasks added after the start, and the counts of finished work and of unfinished work with a `list` of it. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/hours` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open and a list the caller can see. Reads `sprintId` (id) from the body. Answers with `data`: `plannedMinutes`, `loggedMinutes` and `overdueMinutes`, the minutes the sprint is projected to run over its plan. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sprints/report` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs a project the caller can open and a list the caller can see. Reads `sprintId` (id) from the query; a list that is not a sprint answers a failed status. Answers with `data`: the sprint's state, goal and dates, and the totals of what was committed, completed and left unfinished, with the tasks added after the start and those moved out. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/scrum` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs write access to the list's project and the permission to create lists there; a person must make the call. Reads `sprintId` (id), `isScrum` (boolean; false turns Scrum off), `goal` (string), `startDate` and `endDate` (dates, both or neither, the end after the start) from the body. A completed sprint cannot be edited, and a running one cannot have Scrum turned off. Answers with `data`: the sprint. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sprints/start` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs write access to the list's project and the permission to create lists there; a person must make the call. Reads `sprintId` (id) from the body. The list must be a sprint with a start and end date, and a project runs one sprint at a time; the tasks in it become the sprint's commitment. Answers with `data`: the started sprint, or a failed status with the reason. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/config` | Session or token | internal | Reads the workspace's single sign-on settings. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/sso/config` | Session or token | internal | Changes the workspace's single sign-on settings. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sso/config/verify-domain` | Session or token | internal | Checks the DNS record of a single sign-on domain. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/discover` | No login guard | internal | Finds the single sign-on workspace for an email's domain. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/oidc/callback` | No login guard | internal | Finishes an OpenID Connect sign-in. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/oidc/initiate` | No login guard | internal | Starts an OpenID Connect sign-in. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/public` | No login guard | internal | What the sign-in page needs to offer single sign-on. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/sso/saml/acs` | No login guard | internal | Receives the SAML assertion. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/saml/initiate` | No login guard | internal | Starts a SAML sign-in. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/sso/saml/metadata` | No login guard | internal | The SAML service provider metadata. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/support-mail` | Session or token | internal | Sends a message to support from the web app. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/task-templates` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `projectId` (id, optional) from the query: with it, the workspace's templates and that project's, for a project the caller can open; without it, the workspace's templates and those of every project the caller can open. Answers with `data`, sorted by name, each with `name`, `scope`, `projectId`, `isDefault`, `canManage` and a count of what it carries. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/task-templates` | Session or token | undocumented | Allowed for owners and admins when `scope` is `workspace`, and otherwise for those who can create tasks in the task's project. Reads `name` (string, required), `scope` (`project` or `workspace`, `project` when left out), `taskId` (id of the task to copy from, required) and `include` (an object of booleans, where `false` leaves a part out) from the body. Answers with `data`: the template. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v2/task-templates/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Allowed for owners and admins on a workspace template, and for those who can create tasks in the project on a project template. Reads `name` (string, required) from the body. Answers with `statusText` `Template renamed.` and no `data`. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/task-templates/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Allowed for owners and admins on a workspace template, and for those who can create tasks in the project on a project template. The template is marked deleted and is no longer any project's default. Answers with `statusText` `Template deleted.` and no `data`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/task-templates/:id/apply` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to create tasks in the task's project; fields the caller's role may not change are skipped and named. Reads `taskId` (id, required), `overwrite` and `preview` (booleans) from the body; with `preview` true nothing is written. Answers with `data`: `applied`, `conflicts`, `skipped`, `checklistAdded` and `subtasksCreated`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/task-templates/default` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change the project's details. Reads `projectId` (id, required) and `templateId` (id, or empty to clear the default) from the body; the template must be a workspace one or that project's. Answers with `statusText` saying whether a default was set or cleared. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/tasks/bulk` | Session or token | undocumented | Runs one change on many tasks at once. The caller must be able to open every task in `taskIds` and hold the permission for the chosen action in their projects, for example `task.task_status` for `bulkUpdateStatus`, `task.task_delete` for `bulkDelete`, `task.task_archive` for `bulkArchive`, `task.task_move` for `bulkMove`. Reads `action` (string, must start with `bulk` and be a known bulk action such as `bulkUpdateStatus`, `bulkUpdatePriority`, `bulkUpdateDueDate`, `bulkUpdateStartDate`, `bulkUpdateDates`, `bulkUpdateAssignee`, `bulkUpdateTags`, `bulkArchive`, `bulkRestore`, `bulkDelete`, `bulkTrash`, `bulkMove`, `bulkConvertToSubTask`, `bulkConvertToTask`, `bulkAddToList`, `bulkDuplicate`) and `taskIds` (list of ids) from the body, plus the fields that action takes, such as `newStatus`, `firebaseObj`, `DueDate`, `startDate`, `employeeId`, `tagId`, `sprintObj`, `projectData`, `parentTaskId`. Answers with `data`: `updated`, `skipped` (task id and reason), `errors`, `totals` (counts of each), and statusText 'Bulk operation completed'. A bad or unknown action answers `status` false with 'Invalid bulk action' or 'Unknown bulk action'. A task or destination the caller cannot open answers 404, a missing permission answers 403. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/tasks/relations` | Session or token | undocumented | Links two tasks, unlinks them, or lists a task's links. The caller must be able to open the task, with the `task.task_list` permission in its project. Reads `action` (string: `add`, `remove`, `list` or `openBlockers`) and `companyId` from the body, then `taskId` (id, required), and for `add` and `remove` also `relatedTaskId` (id); for `add` it also reads `type` (one of `blocks`, `blocked_by`, `duplicates`, `duplicated_by`, `relates_to`). Answers with the handler's own envelope: `status`, `statusText` and `data`. For `add` and `remove`, `data` holds `taskId`, `relatedTaskId` and `relations` (only the links the caller can open). For `list`, `data` is the linked tasks, each with `taskId`, `type`, `label`, `createdBy`, `createdAt` and a short `task` summary. For `openBlockers`, `data` is the `blocked_by` links whose blocking task is still open. Failures such as a missing task or two tasks already linked answer `status` false with the error text as statusText. An unknown `action` answers `status` false with 'Invalid relation action'. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/test` | Session or token | internal | Used by the web app. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timeTracker/start` | Session or token | internal | Starts a tracker timer on a task. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. The caller can only start a timer for themselves; a `userId` naming someone else answers 403. Reads `description`, `projectId` and `taskId` from the body, all required. A start in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText`: the id of the new time entry. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/:id/review` | Session or token | undocumented | Approves, rejects or reopens a submitted timesheet period. Only an owner or admin may call it; others get `status` false. Reads `id` (the approval record id) from the path and `action` (`approve`, `reject` or `reopen`) and `reason` (string, needed to reject) from the body. The record must be in a state that allows the action. An owner or admin may approve their own week, and the record is marked as self approved. Answers with `statusText` like `Timesheet approved.` and `data`: the updated record. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/bulk-review` | Session or token | undocumented | Approves or rejects several submitted timesheet periods at once. Only an owner or admin has any effect; for others every id is skipped as `not_allowed`. Reads `ids` (a list of record ids, 1 to 100) and `action` (`approve` or `reject`) from the body, both required, plus `reason` (required to reject). Bad input answers 400. Answers with `data`: `results` (one `id` and `outcome` each, `skipped` ones with a `reason`) and `counts` (`approved`, `sent_back`, `skipped`). |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/mine` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists a person's timesheet submissions, newest period first. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `userId` from the query. Only an owner or admin may read another person's list; for anyone else `userId` is ignored and their own is read. Answers with `data`: the approval records. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/pending` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists submitted timesheet periods that wait for review. Only an owner or admin may call it; others get `status` false with `statusText` `Only an owner or admin can review timesheets.` Reads nothing. Answers with `data`: the submitted records, oldest first. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/queue` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists submitted timesheet periods with the context a reviewer needs. Only an owner or admin may call it; others get `status` false. Reads `hoursPerDay` (a number, default 8) from the query. Answers with `data`: the submitted records, oldest first, each with `userName`, `userAvatar`, `totalMinutes`, `entryCount`, `billableMinutes`, `nonBillableMinutes`, `capacityMinutes` and `overMinutes`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/status` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads the approval record of one timesheet period. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `periodStart` and `periodEnd` (dates, required) and `userId` from the query. Only an owner or admin may read another person's record; for anyone else `userId` is ignored and their own is read. Answers with `data`: the approval record, or null when none exists. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timesheet-approval/submit` | Session or token | undocumented | Sends a timesheet period in for approval. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. A member submits their own sheet; an owner or admin may pass another member's `userId`, otherwise it is refused. Reads `periodStart` and `periodEnd` (dates, required), `periodType` (string, default `week`), `note` (string) and `userId` from the body. A period that is already approved is refused. It saves the period as submitted with the minutes and entry count logged in it. Answers with `statusText` `Timesheet submitted for approval.` and `data`: the approval record. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timetracker/can-start` | Session or token | internal | Tells whether the caller may start a timer now. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing. Answers with `statusText` `OK` when it may, or `status` false with `code` `period_locked` when today falls in an approved, locked timesheet period. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timetracker/capture` | Session or token | internal | Stores a tracker screenshot on a running timer and moves its end time forward. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `file`, `path`, `companyId`, `timeSheetId`, `key`, `imageName`, `prevscreenShot`, `screenShotTime`, `memoName` and `strokes` (a JSON string) from a form upload; all are required. `companyId` must match the company header, `timeSheetId` must be a timer of the caller that is still running, and `path` must lie in that timer's own capture folder, or it answers 403 (a path already stored answers 409). The image is read from `file` as a base64 string. Answers with `statusText` `Data Update Succesfully`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timetracker/end` | Session or token | internal | Stops a tracker timer and saves the time. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. The timer must belong to the caller (403 otherwise, 404 if not found). Reads `timeSheetId`, `sprintId`, `projectId`, `taskId`, `taskName`, `projectName`, `companyOwnerId`, `dateFormat`, `timeZone` and `strokes` from the body, all required, plus `considerActionTime` and `actionTime`. A timer that began in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText` `Logtime added successfully`. It also writes task history and a notification. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/timetracker/running` | Session or token | internal | Lists the caller's open tracker timers. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `timeZone` (a zone name) from the query. Answers with `data`: up to 20 timers, newest first, each with `timeSheetId`, `taskId`, `taskName`, `sprintId`, `projectId`, `projectName`, `projectColor`, `note`, `startedAt`, `recordedMinutes`, `elapsedSec`, `live`, `overnight` and `suggestedMinutes`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timetracker/timelog` | Session or token | internal | Lists the caller's own time entries created in a date range. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Only the caller's own entries are returned; a `userId` naming someone else answers 403. Reads `startDate` and `endDate` (dates, default today) and `type` (the collection name, default the time entries) from the body. Answers with `data`: the matching entries. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/timetracker/trim` | Session or token | internal | Closes an open timer at a set length. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. The timer must be the caller's own unless the caller is an owner or admin. Reads `timeSheetId` (id) and `minutes` (a whole number from 1 to 1440) from the body, both required. A timer in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText` `Timer trimmed.` and `data`: the updated entry. Failures come back with `status` false and a `statusText`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/trash` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `kind` (`projects`, `folders`, `lists`, `tasks` or `docs`, `projects` when left out) from the query. Lists only what the caller could open. Answers with `data`: the trashed items, each with a `title`, a `code` and a `projectId`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/trash/:kind/:id/restore` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs what deleting that kind needed, in the project the item belongs to; a person must make the call; a doc is judged by the doc rules. The path `kind` is `projects`, `folders`, `lists`, `tasks` or `docs`, and `id` an id. Projects and lists come back together with their tasks. Answers with `data`: the `kind` and `id`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/users/favourites` | Session or token | internal | Reads the person's favourites. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/users/favourites` | Session or token | internal | Adds or removes a favourite. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/users/favourites/order` | Session or token | internal | Reorders the person's favourites. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/users/home-cards` | Session or token | internal | Stores the person's home page cards. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/users/nav-preferences` | Session or token | internal | Stores the person's navigation preferences. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/users/onboarding` | Session or token | internal | Stores the person's onboarding progress. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/users/sessions` | Session or token | internal | Lists the person's sessions. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/users/sessions/:sessionId` | Session or token | internal | Ends one of the person's sessions. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/verifyEmail` | No login guard | internal | Verifies an email address from a link. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/view-templates` | Session or token | undocumented | Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Answers with `data`, sorted by name, each template with `name`, `viewType` and `canManage`, which is true for owners and admins. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/view-templates` | Session or token | undocumented | Needs the permission to change the project's views. Reads `projectId` (id), `viewId` (string) and `name` (string) from the body, and keeps the setup of that view as a template; a workspace holds a limited number. Answers with `data`: the template. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v2/view-templates/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Owners and admins only. Reads `name` (string, required) from the body. Answers with `data`: the renamed template. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/view-templates/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Owners and admins only. The template is marked deleted. Answers with `statusText` `Template deleted.` and no `data`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/who-can-see/:kind/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Who can see a project, a list or a page. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/workflows/approvals` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists workflow steps waiting for a decision. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/workflows/definitions` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists workflow definitions. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/definitions` | Session or token | undocumented | Creates a workflow definition. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v2/workflows/definitions/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Changes a workflow definition. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v2/workflows/definitions/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Deletes a workflow definition. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v2/workflows/definitions/:id/enabled` | Session or token | undocumented | Switches a workflow definition on or off. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/dry-run` | Session or token | undocumented | Shows what a workflow would do, without doing it. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs` | Session or token | undocumented | Lists workflow runs. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs` | Session or token | undocumented | Starts a workflow run. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads a workflow run and its steps. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/compensate` | Session or token | undocumented | Undoes a step. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/decide` | Session or token | undocumented | Approves or rejects a step. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/reassign` | Session or token | undocumented | Hands a step's decision to someone else. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/resume` | Session or token | undocumented | Resumes a step. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/retry` | Session or token | undocumented | Retries a step. |
| `POST` | `/api/v2/workflows/runs/:id/steps/:stepId/skip` | Session or token | undocumented | Skips a step. |
| `GET` | `/api/v2/workflows/step-types` | Session or token | undocumented | The step types a workflow can use. |

### Version 1 (the web app's own API)

| Method | Path | Auth | Status | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/v1/activity-log` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/addmilestone` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/admin/checkSendInviatation` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/admin/company` | Session or token | internal | |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/admin/company` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/admin/company/find` | Session or token | internal | |

| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/burndown` | Session or token | internal | Gives the burndown of one list (sprint): how much work is left each day against an ideal line. The caller must be able to open the sprint's project and the sprint itself (a private sprint is only for its people and the admins). Reads `sprintId` (id, required) from the query. Answers with `data`: `sprintName`, `isScrum`, `state`, `goal`, `startDate`, `endDate`, `totalCount`, `totalEstimate`, `totalPoints` and `days` (each with `date`, `remainingCount`, `remainingEstimate`, `remainingPoints`, `ideal`, `idealPoints`), and statusText 'Burndown computed.'. A list with no tasks answers `data` with an empty `days`. A bad id answers `status` false with 'companyId and a valid sprintId are required.', a missing sprint answers `status` false with 'Sprint not found.'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/cfd` | Session or token | internal | Gives the cumulative flow of a project: how many tasks sit in each status group on each day. The caller must be able to open the project, and tasks in private sprints they cannot see are left out. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `from` and `to` (dates, optional, default is the last 30 days, at most 120 days) from the query. Answers with `data`: `days`, each with `date` and the counts `open`, `inprogress`, `onhold`, `close`, and statusText 'CFD computed.' or 'No tasks yet.'. A bad id answers `status` false with 'companyId and a valid projectId are required.'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/milestones` | Session or token | internal | Lists the dated milestones of a project, or of every project the caller can open, with how each one is doing. The caller only sees projects they can open, and tasks in private sprints they cannot see are not counted. Reads `projectId` (id, optional) from the query; a value that is not an id answers `status` false. Answers with `data`: `milestones` (each with `milestoneId`, `name`, `projectId`, `projectName`, `baseline`, `target`, `targetField`, `slipDays`, `tasks`, `doneTasks`, `overdueTasks`, `openTasks`, `signedOff`, `state` of `done`, `missed`, `at-risk` or `on-track`, `lastMove`, `moveCount`) and `totals` (`total`, `onTrack`, `atRisk`, `missed`, `done`), and statusText 'Milestones computed.'. With no visible project it answers statusText 'No projects.' and empty lists. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/provenance` | Session or token | internal | Shows who finished the work in a list (sprint) or a project, people or AI agents, with a cost split. The caller must be able to open the project, and for a sprint also the sprint itself. Reads `sprintId` (id) or `projectId` (id) from the query, one of them is required. Answers with `data`: `sprint` (`id` and `name`, or null), `projectId`, the completion split fields, and `margin` (with `currencySymbol`), and statusText 'Provenance computed.'. A missing or bad id answers `status` false with 'companyId and a valid sprintId or projectId are required.', a missing sprint answers `status` false with 'Sprint not found.'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/sprint-insights` | Session or token | internal | Gives extra facts for one list (sprint): which tasks are blocked and for how long, and which tasks were added after the sprint started. The caller must be able to open the sprint's project and the sprint itself. Reads `sprintId` (id, required) from the query. Answers with `data`: `sprintId`, `sprintName`, `projectId`, `startDate`, `endDate`, `blockers` (with `blockedDays` and `since`), `scopeAdds` (with `action`, `by`, `at`, `done`), `tasks` (each with `done`, `blocked`, `committed`) and `hasCommitment`, and statusText 'Sprint insights computed.'. A bad id answers `status` false with 'companyId and a valid sprintId are required.', a missing sprint answers `status` false with 'Sprint not found.'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/agile/velocity` | Session or token | internal | Gives the velocity of a project: points promised against points finished across its latest closed Scrum sprints, with a rolling average of three. The caller must be able to open the project, and private sprints they are not on are left out unless they are owner or admin. Reads `projectId` (id, required) and `limit` (a number, between 1 and 50, default 10) from the query. Answers with `data`: `sprints` (each with `sprintId`, `name`, `startDate`, `endDate`, `committed`, `completed`, `completedHuman`, `completedAgent`, `unchecked`, `byPattern`, `rollingAvg`) and `skipped` (closed sprints without a start snapshot), and statusText 'Velocity computed.' or 'No completed sprints yet.'. A bad id answers `status` false with 'companyId and a valid projectId are required.'. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/comments` | Session or token | internal | Saves a new comment or reply on a project, list or task. The caller must be able to open that project, list and task, else 404 with `statusText`. Reads `data` (object, required) from the body, with `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `message`, `mediaURL` and `parentId` (ids and text). The author is always the caller, whatever `userId` is sent. Mentions in `message` are found and notified, unread counts are raised and agents or AI may start when named. Answers with `status` and `data`: the saved comment, plus `ai` or `agents` when those started. Bad thread ids answer 400, and failures 500. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/comments` | Session or token | internal | Edits or soft-deletes a comment. The caller must be able to open its thread, else 404. Only the author or an owner or admin may change it, but any member may change only `pinnedMessage`, else 403 with `message`. Reads `id` (comment id, required), `data` (object of fields to set), `isProjectComment` (boolean) and `options` (only `timestamps`, a boolean) from the body. A comment cannot move to another thread or author, answering 400. Answers with `status` and `data`: the updated comment. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/comments/action-items` | Session or token | internal | Lists the open assigned comments on one task, oldest first, up to 100. The caller must be able to open the task, else 404. Reads `taskId` (id, required, else 400), `projectId` (id) and `sprintId` (id) from the query. Answers with `data`: the comments. Failures answer 500. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/comments/assign` | Session or token | internal | Assigns a task comment to a member, or clears its assignee. The caller must be able to post to the task, else 404. Changing an existing assignment is allowed only to the comment's author, the one who assigned it, the current assignee or an owner or admin, else 403 with `statusText`. Reads `id` (comment id, required, else 400) and `assigneeId` (user id, empty to clear) from the body. The assignee must be an active person who can see the task, else 400. Answers with `status` and `data`: the updated comment. The new assignee is notified. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/comments/assigned-to-me` | Session or token | internal | Lists the open comments assigned to the caller, newest assignment first, up to 50. Only those on tasks and docs the caller can still open are returned. Reads nothing from the request. Answers with `data`: the comments, each with `taskName` and `taskKey` for task comments. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/comments/get-paginated-messages` | Session or token | internal | Lists the top level comments of a project, list or task chat, newest first. The caller must be able to open the project, list and task, else 404 with `statusText`. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `taskId` (id), `sprintId` (id), `isDefault`, `mainChat` (booleans), `skipValue` and `batchLimit` (numbers, default 0 and 25) and `tabLeaveTime` (a timestamp in milliseconds) from the query. Answers with `data`: the comments, with a reply summary where threads apply. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/comments/get-searched-messages` | Session or token | internal | Searches the messages of a project, list or task chat. The caller must be able to open the project, list and task, else 404 with `statusText`. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `sprintId` and `taskId` (ids), `searchText` (string, matched against message and file names), `isPinnedMessage` (`true` for pinned only), `skip` and `limit` (numbers, default 0 and 25) and `sort` (`asc` or `desc`) from the query. Answers with `data`: the matching comments. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/comments/replies` | Session or token | internal | Lists the replies in one comment thread, oldest first. The caller must be able to open the thread's project, list and task, else 404 with `statusText`. Reads `parentId` (comment id, required, else 400) from the query. Answers with `data`: the replies, and `root`: the parent comment. Failures answer 500. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/comments/resolve` | Session or token | internal | Marks an assigned task comment as resolved or open again. The caller must be able to open the task, else 404. Only the assignee, the person who assigned it or an owner or admin may do it, else 403 with `statusText`. Reads `id` (comment id, required, else 400) and `resolved` (boolean, true unless `false`) from the body. The comment must be assigned, else 400. Answers with `status` and `data`: the updated comment. Failures answer 500. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/createproject` | Session or token | internal | Creates a new project, with its first list, in the workspace. Needs the `project.project_create` permission, and any people named in `AssigneeUserId`, `LeadUserId` or `watchers` must be members. Reads from the body `ProjectName` (required), `ProjectCode` (required), `source` (required), `proposalId` (required when the source is upwork), `projectIcon`, `isPrivateSpace` (true or false), `isTemplate`, `ProjectRequiredComponent` and other project fields. The workspace and creator are set from the session, so a body naming another company is refused with 403. Answers with `data`: the new project, and `statusText`: "createProject added successfully". Failures answer `status` false with `statusText` giving the reason. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/estimatedTime` | Session or token | internal | Runs a read query over planned time rows and returns the result. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `queryeta` (an aggregation pipeline) from the body. The query is limited to the rows and projects the caller may see, and a query that breaks that limit answers 400. Answers with the list of result rows itself, not wrapped in `data`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/estimatedTime` | Session or token | internal | Saves the planned time for one person, task and day. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. A member may plan their own time only; planning for others needs an owner or admin role or the Everyone grant on the workload or project timesheet. The project must be one the caller can open, else 403. Reads `userId`, `taskId`, `projectId` (ids), `date` and `minutes` (a number from 0 to 1440) from the body, all required, plus `id` (the row id, to edit a row) and `timeZone`. Without `id` it creates the row or updates the one for that person, day and task. Answers with the saved row itself, not wrapped in `data`; bad input answers 400. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/estimatedTime/:pid/:tid` | Session or token | internal | Lists the planned time rows for one task. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Callers without the Everyone grant on the workload or project timesheet see only their own rows, and only for projects they can open; a project they cannot open gives an empty list. Reads `pid` (project id) and `tid` (task id) from the path. Answers with the list of rows itself, not wrapped in `data`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/estimatedTime/ai/:tid` | Session or token | internal | Asks the AI for an estimate of a task and saves it on the task. Needs a task the caller can open and the permission to change task estimated hours in its project, else 403. Reads `tid` (task id) from the path and `userId` and `userName` (strings) from the body. The task must have a description. Answers with `data`: `totalEstimatedTime`, `minutes`, `optimistic`, `likely`, `pessimistic`, `confidence`, `work_items`, `reasoning` and `basedOnSamples`. Failures give `status` false and a `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/estimatedTime/ai/:tid/propose` | Session or token | internal | Asks the AI for an estimate of a task without saving it. Needs a task the caller can open and the permission to change task estimated hours in its project, else 403. Reads `tid` (task id) from the path. The task must have a description. Answers with `data`: `minutes`, `optimistic`, `likely`, `pessimistic`, `confidence`, `work_items`, `reasoning`, `basedOnSamples` and `previousMinutes`. Failures give `status` false and a `statusText`. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/folder` | Session or token | internal | Creates a folder in a project, or a category in a chat space. The caller needs `project.project_folder_create` in the project (chat categories are judged by chat access instead). AI agent accounts are refused. Reads `projectId` (id), `folderName` (string), `parentFolderId` (id of a live folder of the same project, optional, folders nest one level) and `mainChat` (boolean) from the body. The acting user is taken from the session. Answers with `data`: the new folder, and statusText 'Folder added successfully'. A bad parent answers 400 with the reason, other failures answer `status` false with the error text. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/folder/:id` | Session or token | internal | Renames, moves, archives, deletes or restores one folder, depending on `type`. The caller must be able to open the folder's project and needs the permission for the change: `project.project_folder_name_edit` for a rename, that key or `project.project_folder_create` for a move, `project.folder_archive`, `project.folder_delete` or `project.folder_restore` for the matching `deletedStatusKey`. AI agent accounts are refused. Reads `id` (folder id) from the path and `type` (string, one of `editFolderName`, `updateFolder`, `moveFolder`, required) from the body. For `editFolderName` it reads `folderName` and `projectId`, and also renames the folder copy kept on its tasks. For `moveFolder` it reads `parentFolderId` (an id, or null for the top level; required). For `updateFolder` it reads `updateObject`, which may only be `{ $set: { deletedStatusKey } }` with 0, 1 or 2, and it carries sub folders and their tasks along. Answers with `data`: the folder, and statusText 'Folder renamed successfully', 'Folder moved successfully' or 'Folder updated successfully' (`updateFolder` also returns `subfolders`). A missing `type` answers `status` false with 'type not found', an unknown `type` answers 400 'Invalid type', a folder that is not found answers `status` false with 'Folder not found'. |

| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/importTasks` | Session or token | internal | Creates many tasks in one list from imported rows. The caller needs `task.task_create` in the project named by `projectData`, and must be able to see the target list (a private list is only for its people and the admins). AI agent accounts are refused. Reads `tasks` (list of task rows), `projectData` (object with `_id` and `CompanyId`), `sprint` (object with `id`, the list to import into), `statusArray` (list of statuses with `name`, `key`, `type`), `indexObj`, `eventId` (a string used to report progress) and `userData` from the body. The list must belong to the project, else it answers 404 'Project or sprint not found.'. A missing permission answers 403 'You do not have permission to import tasks into this project.'. On success answers with `data`: `createdTasks`, `customFields`, `adjusted`, `droppedFieldValues`, and statusText 'Task updated successfully.'. |

| `GET` | `/api/v1/members` | Session or token | internal | Lists the member rows of the workspace. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the fields on each row depend on the caller: owners, admins and holders of `settings.settings_member_list` see seat details, others see a smaller set, and `userId` is hidden on rows that are not accepted yet. Reads nothing from the request. Answers with `data`: the member rows, and sets a `FromCache` header when cached. Failures answer 500. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/members` | Session or token | internal | Changes fields on a member row, such as role, seat status, removal, manager or designation. Needs the `settings.settings_member_list` permission, except that any member may change their own `dashboardLocked` setting. Reads `id` (member row id, required) and `data` (object, required) from the body. Only an owner or admin can change another member or `managerId`. Only an owner can grant the owner or admin role or change an owner. Nobody can change their own role, and the last owner cannot be demoted or removed. Fields such as `userId`, `companyId` and `userEmail` cannot be changed, and `$` operators are refused. A manager must pass the reporting line check. Removing a member releases their seat and revokes their API tokens. Answers with `status`, `statusText` (`Member updated.`) and `data`: the updated row. Refusals answer 400, 403 or 404 with `statusText`, and other failures 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/members/:id` | Session or token | internal | Finds one member row by account id. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `id` (the member's user id) from the path. Managers of members can find any row of that account, everyone else only finds a live seat. Answers with the member row itself (no `data` wrapper), or an empty answer when none matches. Failures answer 500. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/members/:key/:value` | Session or token | internal | Checks whether any member holds a given role or designation. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `key` (`roleType` or `designation`) and `value` (a whole number) from the path. Anything else answers 400 with `message`. Answers with `isUsed`: true when a member has it. Failures answer 500. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/members/count` | Session or token | internal | Counts member rows that match a simple filter. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `query` (object) from the body, which may use only `roleType`, `designation`, `status` and `isDelete`, each a string, number or boolean. Anything else answers 400 with `statusText`. Answers with a list holding `totalCount` (or an empty list when nothing matches), with no `data` wrapper. Failures answer 500. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/members/private-view` | Session or token | internal | Adds, changes or removes a private view saved on the caller's own member row. Any signed-in member may call it, but only on their own row, else 403 with `statusText`. Reads `id` (member row id, required), `data` (object, required), `operation` (`push`, `update`, `settings` or `delete`, required) and `key` (`name`, `title` or `isPin`, used with `update`) from the body. `update` and `settings` also need `data.id` (the view id) and the view must exist, else 404. Answers with `status` and `statusText` (`Private view saved.`). Bad input answers 400, an unknown row 404, and other failures 500. It also records a note in the project history when the view names a project the caller can read. |

| `GET` | `/api/v1/project-close` | Session or token | internal | Reads the workspace policy for closing inactive projects. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing from the body or query. Answers with `data`: `policy` and `validInactiveMonths`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project-close` | Session or token | internal | Changes the workspace policy for closing inactive projects. Only an owner or an admin may call it, otherwise it answers 403. Reads `enabled` (true or false) and `inactiveMonths` (a number from the allowed list) from the body, and needs at least one of them. Answers with `data`: `policy` as saved, and `statusText`: "Auto-close settings updated". A bad value answers 400 with `statusText`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project-dashboard/:projectId` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project-status-template` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project-status-template` | Session or token | internal | |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project-status-template` | Session or token | internal | |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/project-status-template/:id` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads one project by its id. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project/:id` | Session or token | internal | Updates fields of one project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members need the permission tied to each field they change (for example the name, dates, status, assignee or description permissions), and `project.project_details` for fields without their own key. Reads `id` (id) from the path and `updateObject` (object, required), `key` (one of $set, $unset, $push, $pull, $addToSet; default $set), `arrayFilters` (list) and `timeZone` (string) from the body. Server owned fields such as the creator, company or counters cannot be changed and answer 400, and moving a project is refused for agent tokens. People named must be members. Answers with the project record. Failures answer 400 with `message` (for example a bad source or proposal id) or 500. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project/:id/view-settings` | Session or token | internal | Saves the settings of one view of a project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.view_list` or `project.project_details` permission. Reads `id` (id) from the path and `viewId` (string, required) and `settings` (object, required) from the body. Answers with `data`: `viewId` and the cleaned `settings`, and `statusText`: "View saved.". An unknown view or project answers 404. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/:id/views` | Session or token | internal | Adds a new view to a project, copied from an existing view or built from a view template. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.view_list` or `project.project_details` permission. Reads `id` (id) from the path. In the body it reads either `templateId` (with optional `title`, `isPrivate`, `isPin`) or `sourceViewId` (string, required) with `title` (required) and optional `settings` (object). Answers with `data`: the new view (or a prepared private view), `leftOut`, and `statusText`: "View added." A project holds at most 60 views. An unknown view or template answers 404, and a bad body answers 400. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project/allTask/:id` | Session or token | internal | Changes the deleted state of tasks in a project, used when a project is closed, deleted, archived or restored. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members need the `project.project_delete` or `project.project_close` permission, and agent tokens are refused. Reads `id` (id) from the path and `findObject` (required; only `ProjectID` and `deletedStatusKey`) and `updateObject` (required; only `deletedStatusKey`, a whole number) from the body. Answers with `data`: `matched` and `modified`, and `statusText`: "Project tasks updated." Bad input answers 400 with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/checklist` | Session or token | internal | Adds, changes or removes items of a project's checklist. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members need the `project.project_checklist` permission; adding or removing an assignee on an item can be done with either `project.project_checklist_assign_remove` or `project.project_checklist`. Reads `id` (the project id), `operation` (push, update or delete), `checklistItem` (an item, or a list of item ids for delete) and `key` (name, isChecked, assigneeAdd or assigneeRemove, for update) from the body. An update by name or assignee needs `checklistItem.id`. Answers `status` true. An unknown project answers 404 and a bad `operation` answers 400 with `message`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project/filter/:userId` | Session or token | internal | Lists the caller's saved project filters. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but `userId` (id, from the path) must be the caller's own id or it answers 403. Answers with `data`: the caller's saved filters of the project kind. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/filter/create` | Session or token | internal | Saves a project filter for the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the filter always belongs to the caller. Reads `name` (string, required, up to 200 characters), `filter` (string, required), `typeFilter` (string, required), `filters` (list), `sortByField` and `sortByOrder` (objects) and `userId` (must be the caller's own) from the body. Answers with `data`: the saved filter, and `statusText`: "Filter saved." A bad field answers 400 with `statusText` and `field`, and another user's id answers 403. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/project/filter/delete/:cid/:id` | Session or token | internal | Deletes one saved filter of the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but only the owner of the filter can delete it. Reads `cid` (id, must match the workspace of the request) and `id` (id, the filter) from the path, and an optional `projectId` from the query for the history. Answers `status` true. A wrong company answers 403, a bad id 400, and a filter that is not the caller's or does not exist 404. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project/filter/update` | Session or token | internal | Changes one saved project filter of the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but only the owner of the filter can change it. Reads `id` (id, required; `_id` also works) and at least one of `name`, `filters` (list), `sortByField`, `sortByOrder` (objects) from the body, and `userId` if sent must be the caller's own. Answers with `data`: the updated filter, and `statusText`: "Filter updated." A bad field answers 400, another user's id 403, and a filter that is not found 404. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/personal` | Session or token | internal | Finds the caller's personal list project, or creates it the first time. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it only ever returns the caller's own personal project. Reads nothing from the body or query. Answers with `data`: `project` and `sprint` (its first list), and `statusText` is "Personal list created" or "Personal list found". Failures answer 400 with `message`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/search` | Session or token | internal | Searches the projects, sprints, folders or archived lists the caller can see. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and results only include projects the caller may open (private ones only if assigned or if their role sees all private projects). Reads `type` (required; one of sprint, folder, projectName, projectFilter, projectFilter_projectName, projectFilter_folder, projectFilter_sprint, showArchiveOnly) from the body, plus `search` (string), `fields` (comma separated string), `query`, `sortByField`, `sortByOrder`, `showArchived`, `skip` and `limit` (numbers). Answers with the list of matches itself (no `data` wrapper), or an empty list if the caller has no active seat. A missing `type` answers 404 with `message`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/project/sprint/:id` | Session or token | internal | Adds or removes the caller's own favourite star on a list (sprint). Needs access to the sprint and its project, and no extra permission for this own star. Reads `id` (id) from the path and `key` ($addToSet or $pull) and `updateObject` from the body, which must be exactly `{ favouriteTasks: { userId } }` with the caller's own id. Anything else answers 400. Answers with the updated sprint. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project/sprintFolder/:id` | Session or token | internal | Lists the sprints or folders of a project, or counts them. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner). Reads `id` (id) from the path and `collection` (required; sprints or folders), `count`, `fields` (comma separated string), `skip` and `limit` from the query. Sprints the caller may not see are left out unless the caller is an owner or admin, and removed ones are left out. Answers with the list itself (no `data` wrapper), or a count when `count` is set. A missing or unknown `collection` answers 400 with `message`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/tags` | Session or token | internal | Adds, renames, recolours or removes a tag defined on a project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members need the `task.task_tag` permission. Reads `id` (the project id), `operation` (push, update or delete), `items` (the tag; for update and delete it needs `id`, the tag's `uid`) and `key` (tagName or tagColor, for update) from the body. Answers `status` true. An unknown project answers 404 and a bad `operation` answers 400 with `message`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/project/template/custom` | Session or token | internal | Lists the custom project templates of the workspace. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing from the body or query. Answers with `data`: the templates (served from a cache when one is fresh). |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/template/custom` | Session or token | internal | Saves a custom project template for the workspace. Only an owner or an admin may call it, otherwise it answers 403. Reads `data` (object, required) from the body; `data.TemplateName` is required, a string of up to 250 characters. Answers with `data`: the saved template, and `statusText`: "Template saved." A bad name answers 400 with `statusText` and `field`. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/project/template/custom/:id` | Session or token | internal | Deletes one custom project template. Only an owner or an admin may call it, otherwise it answers 403. Reads `id` (id) from the path. Answers `status` true, or `status` false with 404 when nothing was deleted. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/project/template/custom/ai-generate` | Session or token | internal | Asks the AI to draft task statuses, task types and project statuses for a project template, without saving anything. Only an owner or an admin may call it, otherwise it answers 403. Reads `category` (string, required) and `useCaseDescription` (string) from the body. Answers with `data`: the draft template (`taskStatusData`, `TemplateTaskType`, `projectStatusData`, `finalTemplateTaskStatusData`, `finalProjectStatusData`), matched to the workspace's existing statuses and types. A missing `category` answers 400, and so does an AI that is not set up. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/projectRules/:pid` | Session or token | internal | |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/projectRules/delete/:pid` | Session or token | internal | |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/projectRules/update` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/autoArchive` | Session or token | internal | Sets the rule that archives finished tasks of a project after some days. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.project_details` permission for that project. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `enabled` (true or false) and `afterDays` (a number, kept between 1 and 365) from the body. Answers with `data`: `enabled` and `afterDays` as saved. A missing project answers `status` false with `statusText`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/autoArchive/:pid` | Session or token | internal | Reads the auto archive rule of a project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner). Reads `pid` (id) from the path. Answers with `data`: `enabled` (true or false) and `afterDays` (days, 1 to 365, default 30). A missing project answers `status` false with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/estimationScale` | Session or token | internal | Sets the story point scale a project offers. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.project_details` permission for that project. Reads `projectId` (id, required) and `scale` (one of fibonacci, linear, tshirt, hours; anything else becomes fibonacci) from the body. Answers with `data`: `estimationScale`. A missing project answers `status` false with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/migrateSprintsFun` | Instance admin | internal | Runs a one time migration that turns the sprints and folders stored inside every project and chat of the workspace into their own records. Only the instance owner may call it, or a script that sends the `adminkey` header; an API token is refused. Reads nothing from the body or query. Answers `status` true with `statusText`: "done" once every project was processed. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/taskStatus` | Session or token | internal | Moves every task in the old statuses of a project over to the new status chosen for each one. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.project_details` permission for that project. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `taskStatusKey` (list, required) and `oldTaskStatus` (list, required; each item has `key` and `convertStatus` with `key`, `name` and `type`) from the body. Answers `status` true with `statusText`: "TASK UPDATE SUCCESFULLY". A missing field answers `status` false with a `statusText` naming it. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/taskStatus/wipLimit` | Session or token | internal | Sets or clears the work in progress limit of one status column in a project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.project_details` permission for that project. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `statusKey` (required) and `wipLimit` (a number) from the body. A value that is empty, zero or below clears the limit, and the limit tops out at 999. Answers with `data`: `statusKey`, `wipLimit`, `taskStatusData`, and `statusText` says the limit was set or cleared. Failures answer `status` false with `statusText` (bad project id, project not found, status not on the project). |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/projectSetting/taskType` | Session or token | internal | Moves every task of the old task types in a project over to the new type chosen for each one. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner), and owners and admins always pass. Other members also need the `project.project_details` permission for that project. Reads `projectId` (id, required), `taskTypeKey` (list, required) and `oldTaskType` (list, required; each item has `key` and `convertType` with `key` and `value`) from the body. Answers `status` true with `statusText`: "TASK UPDATE SUCCESFULLY". A missing field answers `status` false with a `statusText` naming it. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/projectTabs` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/projectdata/taskData` | Session or token | internal | Reads one task with its project, list, folder and its subtasks. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner). The caller must also be able to read the task, or it answers 404. Reads `taskId` (id, required), `projectId` (id, required) and `subTaskLimit` (a number, required) from the query. Answers with the list result itself (no `data` wrapper): the project row with `tasks`, `subtasks` (newest first, up to `subTaskLimit`) and `parentFolder`. A missing or malformed parameter answers 400 with `message`. |

| `PUT` | `/api/v1/push-mark-read` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/pushupdateunreadcommentscount` | Session or token | internal | Changes the signed-in user's unread count for a project, task, chat message, mention or notification. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but only for their own counts: if `userIds` (array of ids) names anyone else it answers 403 with `statusText`. Reads `key` (number, required: 1 project, 2 task, 3 chat message, 4 mention, 5 notification), `read`, `set` and `readAll` (booleans), `messageCount` and `prevCount` (numbers), `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `messageId` and `parentTaskId` (ids) from the body. Keys 1 and 2 need `projectId` and `userIds`, key 2 also needs `sprintId` and `taskId`, key 3 needs `messageId`, key 4 needs `readAll`. With `read`, `set` or `readAll` it changes only the caller's count; without them only keys 1, 2 and 4 are accepted, else 403. A `companyId` in the body that differs from the company header is refused. Answers with `status` and `data`: a message that the count was updated. A rejected request answers 400 with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks` | Session or token | internal | Creates a repeating task rule for a project. The caller needs `task.task_create` in the project named by `projectData`. Reads `name` (string, required), `taskName` (string, required), `projectData` (object with `_id` required, plus `CompanyId`, `ProjectCode`, `ProjectName`), `freq` (required, `daily`, `weekly` or `monthly`), `interval` (a number), `byweekday` (list of numbers 0 to 6), `monthday` (a number), `runHour` (a number, default 9), `skipIfOpen` (boolean), `missedPolicy` (`skip`, `create` or `roll`), `until` (a date), `sprintId` or `sprintArray`, `assignees` (list of user ids), `taskType`, `taskTypeKey`, `priority`, `points`, `rawDescription`, `descriptionBlock` and `userData` from the body. A missing required field answers `status` false with 'Missing required fields (name, taskName, projectData, freq)'. Answers with `data`: the saved rule, and statusText 'Recurring task created'. A description that is too long answers 400. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/:id` | Session or token | internal | Changes a repeat rule. The caller must be able to write in the rule's project (no task permission key is checked here). Reads `id` (rule id) from the path and any of `name`, `enabled`, `freq`, `interval`, `byweekday`, `monthday`, `runHour`, `skipIfOpen`, `missedPolicy` and `until` from the body; when the schedule fields change the next run time is worked out again. Answers with `status` true and statusText 'Updated'. A failure answers `status` false with the error text. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/:id` | Session or token | internal | Deletes a repeat rule by switching it off and marking it deleted. The caller must be able to write in the rule's project (no task permission key is checked here). Reads `id` (rule id) from the path. Answers with `status` true and statusText 'Deleted'. A failure answers `status` false with the error text. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/:id/run-now` | Session or token | internal | Makes one task from a repeat rule right now. The caller needs `task.task_create` in the rule's project. Reads `id` (rule id) from the path. Answers with `data`: `id` (the new task id or null) and `skipped` (true when the last task is still open and the rule's policy says not to make another), and statusText 'Task created' or 'Skipped, previous instance still open'. A rule that is not found answers `status` false with 'Definition not found'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/project/:pid` | Session or token | internal | Lists the recurring task rules of one project. Needs a project the caller can open (a private project only for people assigned to it or roles that see every private project; a personal list only for its owner). Reads `pid` (id) from the path. Answers with `data`: the project's recurring task rules that are not deleted. On an error it answers `status` false with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/run-due` | Session or token | internal | Runs every repeat rule of the workspace that is due now. Only an owner or an admin may call it. Reads nothing from the body. Answers with `data`: `processed` (rules looked at) and `created` (tasks made), and statusText 'Processed due recurring tasks'. A failure answers `status` false with the error text. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/task/:taskId` | Session or token | internal | Reads the repeat rule of one task. The caller must be able to open the task's project. Reads `taskId` (id) from the path. Answers with `status` true and `data`: the rule, or null when the task does not repeat. A bad id answers 400 'A valid task id is required.', a task that is not found answers 404 'Task not found.'. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/task/:taskId` | Session or token | internal | Makes a task repeat, or changes how it repeats. The caller needs `task.task_due_date` and `task.task_create` in the task's project. Reads `taskId` (id) from the path and `freq` (required, `daily`, `weekly` or `monthly`), `interval` (whole number from 1 up), `byweekday` (list of days 0 to 6), `monthday` (1 to 31), `runHour` (0 to 23), `until` (a date), `maxRuns` (whole number) and `missedPolicy` (`skip`, `create` or `roll`) from the body. The acting user comes from the session. Answers with `data`: the rule, and statusText 'Repeat set' for a new rule or 'Repeat updated' for an existing one. A bad schedule or id answers 400 with the reason, a task that is not found answers 404 'Task not found.'. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/recurring-tasks/task/:taskId` | Session or token | internal | Stops a task from repeating by removing its repeat rule. The caller needs `task.task_due_date` in the task's project. Reads `taskId` (id) from the path. Answers with `status` true and statusText 'Repeat removed', also when the task had no rule. A bad id answers 400, a task that is not found answers 404 'Task not found.'. |

| `PUT` | `/api/v1/root-members` | Session or token | internal | Lets an invited person accept their invitation to a company. The caller must be signed in, and either holds `settings.settings_member_list` or sends a body that only links their own account to the invitation. Reads `id` (invitation row id, required), `companyId` (id, required), `data` (object with `userId` and `status`, required) and `linkId` (string, the invitation link token) from the body. The `data.userId` must be the caller, `data.status` must be 2, and the invitation must still be pending, carry the right link and be sent to the caller's account or email. Answers with `status`, `statusText` (`Invitation accepted.`) and `data`: the seat row. Refusals answer 400, 401, 403 or 404 with `statusText`, and other failures 500. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/sprint` | Session or token | internal | Creates a list (sprint) in a project, or a chat channel. The caller needs `project.project_sprint_create` in the project (chat channels are judged by chat access instead), and any person named as a member of a channel must be a member of the workspace. AI agent accounts are refused. Reads `projectId` (id), `sprintName` (string), `folder` (object with `folderId`, the folder to put it in, which must be a live folder of the project), `private` (boolean), `icon`, `mainChat` (boolean, for a chat channel), `AssigneeUserId` and `sendMessage` (channels only), `isPreCompany`, `from` and `taskSprintObj` from the body. The acting user is taken from the session. Answers with `data`: the new list, and statusText 'List added successfully'. It answers `status` false with 'Upgrade your plan' (and `isUpgrade` true) when the plan's list limit is reached, or 'Channels creation limits have been exceeded' for channels. Other failures answer `status` false with the error text. |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/sprint/:id` | Session or token | internal | Renames, updates or deletes one list (sprint) or chat channel, depending on `type`. The caller must be able to open the list and write in its project, and needs the permission that matches the change: `project.project_sprint_name_edit` for a rename, `project.sprint_delete` for `deleteChannel`, and for `updateSprint` the restore, delete or archive key for the new `deletedStatusKey`, or the edit or share keys when it moves a list or changes who may see it. AI agent accounts are refused. Reads `id` (list id) from the path and `type` (string, one of `editSprintName`, `updateSprint`, `deleteChannel`, required) from the body. For `editSprintName` it reads `sprintName` (string, 1 to 50 characters) and `projectId`. For `updateSprint` it reads `updateObject`, which may only `$set` `deletedStatusKey`, `private`, `AssigneeUserId`, `folderId`, `folderName`, or `$addToSet` or `$pull` one member or watcher. A list that is running a Scrum sprint cannot be archived. For `deleteChannel` it reads `companyId`. Answers with `data`: the updated list, and statusText 'Sprint_updated_successfully' (or 'Sprint_deleted_successfully' for a delete). A missing `type` answers `status` false with 'type not found', an unknown `type` answers 400 'Invalid type', a list that is not found answers `status` false with 'Sprint not found'. |
