| `POST` | `/api/v1/tabSyncTask` | Session or token | internal | Returns the tasks of one list that changed since the person left the browser tab, so the board or table can refresh. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the rows are limited to tasks the caller may see. Reads `pid` (project id, required), `sprintId` (list id, required), `istableTask` (boolean, required, true for table view and false for board view), `tabLeaveTime` (a time in milliseconds), `item` (the group being refreshed, with `indexName` and `mongoConditions` or `conditions`), `indexName`, `sortKey` (like 'field:1'), `showAllTasks`, `userId` and `parentId` (to read sub tasks of one parent) from the body. Answers with the raw list of matching rows, not wrapped in `data`; for board view it is one object holding `result` (changed tasks) and `count`. A missing required field or a refused query answers 400 with `message`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/task` | Session or token | internal | Moves every task of one project between active, closed, deleted and restored states, to follow a change of the project itself. AI agent accounts are refused. The caller must be a member of the workspace and, unless owner or admin, able to open the project, and must hold the permission for the change: `project.project_close` for 0 to 8, `project.project_delete` for 0 to 7 or 0 to 1, `project.project_list` for 7 to 0 or 8 to 0. Reads `key` (string, must be `updateMany`), `firstParameter` (object with `ProjectID` or `objId.ProjectID`, and `deletedStatusKey` as the current state number) and `secondParameter` (object `{ $set: { deletedStatusKey } }` with the new state number), all required; any other body key is refused. Answers with `data`: the update result (`matchedCount`, `modifiedCount`), and statusText 'Tasks updated successfully.'. A refused request answers 403 with statusText 'Forbidden' and a `message`; a missing field answers 400. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/task/:id` | Session or token | undocumented | Reads one task by its id. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/task/filter/:userId` | Session or token | internal | Lists the saved task filters of the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but only for themselves. Reads `userId` (id) from the path; if it is not the caller's own id it answers 403 'You can only read your own saved filters.'. Answers with `status` true and `data`: the caller's saved filters of kind `taskFilter` for `projectTask`, or an empty list. A failure answers 500 with statusText. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/task/filter/create` | Session or token | internal | Saves a task filter for the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the filter always belongs to the caller. Reads the filter fields from the body, for example `name`, `filters`, `sortByField`, `sortByOrder`; any `_id` is dropped and `userId` and `companyId` are set by the server. A `userId` naming someone else answers 403 'A saved filter can only be created for yourself.'. Answers with `status` true and `data`: the saved filter. A failure answers 500 with statusText. |
| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/task/filter/delete/:cid/:id` | Session or token | internal | Deletes one saved task filter of the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it can only remove filters the caller owns. Reads `cid` (company id, must equal the workspace in the `companyid` header, else 403) and `id` (filter id) from the path, and `projectId` from the query only to write a history line. A bad id answers 400, a filter that is not found for the caller answers 404 'Saved filter not found.'. On success answers `status` true with no `data`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/task/filter/update` | Session or token | internal | Changes one saved task filter of the caller. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and it can only touch filters the caller owns. The body is a list of two items: first `{ _id }` (the filter id, required, 24 character id), then `{ $set }` holding any of `name`, `filters`, `sortByField`, `sortByOrder` (other fields are ignored). Reads `projectId` from the query only to write a history line. A bad id or an empty update answers 400, a filter that is not found for the caller answers 404 'Saved filter not found.'. On success answers `status` true with no `data`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/task/find` | Session or token | internal | Runs a read only search over tasks using a query pipeline and returns the matching tasks. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the server first narrows the rows to tasks the caller may see. Reads `findQuery` (a list of query stages, or one stage, required), `replaceUndefined` (boolean) and `inList` (a list id, so that tasks added to that list as an extra list are included) from the body. Answers with the raw list of matching rows, not wrapped in `data`. A missing query answers 400 'Query is required.', a stage that is not allowed answers 400 'Query refused' with `message` and `stage`, and other failures answer 500. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/taskIndex` | Session or token | internal | Saves the new place of a task after it is dragged on a board, and changes any field the drag changed. The caller must be able to open the task, with the `task.task_list` permission in its project. Reads from the body, all required: `taskId`, `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskKey`, `isFirst` (boolean), `isFirstWithRecord` (boolean), `relevantIndex` (a number), `indexName` (one of `groupByStatusIndex`, `groupByPriorityIndex`, `groupByAssigneeIndex`, `groupByDueDateIndex`), `searchKey` (one of `statusKey`, `Task_Priority`, `AssigneeUserId`, `DueDate`), `relevantKey` and `updateData` (object with the changed `statusKey`, `Task_Priority`, `AssigneeUserId`, `DueDate` and so on). The workspace is taken from the header. The work is queued per project and done after the answer, so it answers right away with `status` true and statusText 'Process Is Start For Index'. A missing field answers 400 '<field> is required'. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/taskPriority` | Session or token | internal | Lists the workspace's task priorities. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing from the body or query; the workspace comes from the `companyid` header. Answers with the raw list of settings documents named for task priorities (not wrapped in `data`), kept in a cache for a week and marked with a `FromCache` header when it came from there. A missing header answers 400, a failure answers 500 with `message`. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/taskPriority` | Session or token | internal | Changes the workspace's list of task priorities. Only an owner or an admin may call it. Reads `key` (string, required, `$push` or `$pull`) and `updateObject` (object, required, holding `settings` with one priority entry) from the body. A missing or invalid value answers 400 with `message`. Answers with the raw update result (not wrapped in `data`), and clears the cached priority list. A failure answers 500 with `message` and `error`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/teams` | Session or token | internal | Lists the teams of the workspace. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing from the request. Answers with the list of team records itself (no `data` wrapper), and sets a `FromCache` header when the list came from cache. Failures answer 500 with `message` and `error`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/teams/addTeam` | Session or token | internal | Creates a team. The caller needs the `settings.settings_create_team` permission, checked strictly, else 403 with `statusText`. Reads `name` (string, required), `value` (string, required), `teamColor` (object with `bgColor` and `color` strings, required) and `assigneeUsersArray` (list of user ids, each must be a member of the workspace) from the body. Any other field is refused. Answers with the saved team record itself. Bad input answers 400 with `statusText`, and other failures 500. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/teams/updateTeam` | Session or token | internal | Changes a team's name or colour, or adds or removes one member. The caller needs the `settings.settings_team_list` permission, checked strictly, else 403 with `statusText`. Reads `id` (team id, required), `key` (`$set`, `$addToSet` or `$pull`, required) and `updateObject` (object) from the body. With `$set`, `updateObject` may hold `name`, `value` (both strings) and `teamColor`. With `$addToSet` or `$pull`, it holds one `assigneeUsersArray` user id, and an added user must be a workspace member. Answers with the updated team record itself. Bad input answers 400 with `statusText`, and other failures 500. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/billable-summary` | Session or token | internal | Adds up billable and non-billable time over a period. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Owners and admins read the whole company and may filter by user; others read only their own time. Projects hidden from the caller are left out. Reads `userArray` (ids), `projectArray` (ids), `start` and `end` (Unix times in seconds, both needed to filter by date) from the body. Answers with `data`: the billable split plus `scope` (`company` or `self`). |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/timesheet/entries/billable` | Session or token | internal | Marks time entries billable or not. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Owners and admins may change any entry; others only their own, and other entries are left alone. Reads `entryIds` (a list of ids, required, else 400) and `billable` (a boolean, anything but false counts as true) from the body. Answers with `statusText` `Billable updated.` and `data`: `updated` and `billable`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/export-csv` | Session or token | internal | Exports time entries as a CSV file. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `userArray` (ids), `projectArray` (ids), `start` and `end` (Unix times in seconds, both needed to filter by date) from the body. Answers with a downloadable CSV file `timesheet-export.csv` with user, project, date, description, billable and hours. A failure answers 500 with the text `Export failed`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/generate-invoice` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/timesheet/hours-by-source` | Session or token | internal | Adds up the hours people logged, leaving out AI agent time. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Owners and admins get the whole company, others only their own hours. Reads `start` and `end` (Unix times in seconds) from the query, both optional. Answers with `data`: `peopleHours`, `entryCount`, `start`, `end` and `scope` (`company` or `self`). |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/logDetail` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/milestone` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/project` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/timesheet/rates` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/rates` | Session or token | internal | Saves a billing rate. Only an owner or admin may call it, else 403. Reads `scope` and `rate` (required), `refId` (needed for scopes other than `default`) and `currency` (default `USD`) from the body. It updates the rate for that scope and `refId` or adds it. Answers with `statusText` `Rate saved.` and `data`: the saved rate; bad input gives `status` false. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/timesheet/reminder-settings` | Session or token | internal | Reads the company's time reminder settings. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads the company header only. Answers with `data`: `settings`. Without the company header it answers 400. |
| `PUT` | `/api/v1/timesheet/reminder-settings` | Session or token | internal | Changes the company's time reminder settings. Only an owner or admin may call it, else 403. Reads `enabled` (a boolean) and `userIds` (a list) from the body; at least one is needed, else 400. Answers with `statusText` `Reminder settings updated.` and `data`: `settings`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/send-reminders` | Session or token | internal | Sends the log your time reminder emails now. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads nothing. It emails the chosen members who have not logged time today, only when reminders are switched on, there are chosen members and today is a working day. Answers with `statusText` `Reminders processed.` and `data`: `reminded`, `total`, `users` and, when nothing was sent, `skipped` with the reason. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/timesheet/task/:taskId` | Session or token | internal | Gives the time entries logged on one task. Needs a task the caller can open. Reads `taskId` from the path. The caller sees only their own entries, unless they hold the Everyone grant on the user timesheet for that project, then all. Answers with `data`: `taskId`, `estimateMinutes`, `totalMinutes`, `mineMinutes`, `seesEveryone` and `entries` (each with `userId`, `startedAt`, `endedAt`, `minutes`, `note`, `billable`, `source`, `running`, `locked` and `canEdit`). A bad id answers 400 and a missing task 404. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/timelog` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/tracker` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/timesheet/user` | Session or token | internal | |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/timesheet/week` | Session or token | internal | Gives one person's week of logged time, grouped by task, with capacity and approval state. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Only an owner or admin may name another person with `userId`; for anyone else it is ignored. Reads `start` and `end` (dates, required, else 400), `userId`, `projectId`, `hoursPerDay` (a number, default 8) and `timeZone` from the query. Answers with `data`: `userId`, `zone`, `hoursPerDay`, `workingDays`, `days`, `rows`, `totals`, `underCapacity`, `approval` and `running`. |

| `POST` | `/api/v1/unsetCommentCounts` | Session or token | internal | Clears stored unread comment counts for a project or list. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, and the code checks no project access or role. Reads `projectId` (id), `sprintId` (id), `searchKey` (string) and `companyId` (id) from the body. One of `projectId` or `searchKey` is required, else 400 with `statusText`. A `companyId` that differs from the company header answers 403. It removes the matching count fields for every user's count record in the company, not only the caller's. Answers with `status`, `statusText` (`success`) and `data`: the result of the bulk update. Failures answer 500 with `statusText`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/updateAiModel` | Instance admin | internal | |
| `PATCH` | `/api/v1/updateBucket/:bucketId` | Instance admin | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/updateEmailTemplate` | Instance admin | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/updateTaskIndexOnload` | Session or token | internal | Gives a task a board position when it has none for the chosen grouping, as the board loads. The caller must be able to open the task, with the `task.task_list` permission in its project. Reads `taskUpdate` (required) from the body: an object with `data` (the task id) and `item` (with `indexName`, `searchKey`, `searchValue`). Answers with `status` true and statusText 'Index Update Successfully' when it set a position, or 'No Need To Processs' when the task already had one. A missing `taskUpdate` answers `status` false with 'Task Update Is Required', and a task that is not found answers 404 'Task not found'. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/updatemilestone` | Session or token | internal | |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/updateunreadcommentscount` | Session or token | internal | Changes the signed-in user's unread count for a project, task, chat message, mention or notification. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it, but only for their own counts: if `userIds` (array of ids) names anyone else it answers 403 with `statusText`. Reads `key` (number, required: 1 project, 2 task, 3 chat message, 4 mention, 5 notification), `read`, `set` and `readAll` (booleans), `messageCount` and `prevCount` (numbers), `projectId`, `sprintId`, `taskId`, `messageId` and `parentTaskId` (ids) from the body. Keys 1 and 2 need `projectId` and `userIds`, key 2 also needs `sprintId` and `taskId`, key 3 needs `messageId`, key 4 needs `readAll`. With `read`, `set` or `readAll` it changes only the caller's count; without them only keys 1, 2 and 4 are accepted, else 403. A `companyId` in the body that differs from the company header is refused. Answers with `status` and `data`: a message that the count was updated. A rejected request answers 400 with `statusText`. |

### Everything else

| Method | Path | Auth | Status | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/` | No login guard | undocumented | |
| `GET` | `/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server` | No login guard | undocumented | |
| `GET` | `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` | No login guard | undocumented | |
| `GET` | `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource/mcp` | No login guard | undocumented | |
| `PATCH` | `/api/tasks/` | Session or token | internal | Changes one task with an older style call that only knows three actions. The caller must be able to open the task and hold the matching task permission in its project: `task.task_status` for `updateStatus`, `task.task_priority` for `updatePriority`, `task.task_name_edit` for `updateTaskName`. Reads `action` (string, one of those three, any other value is refused with 400) from the body, plus the fields of that action: `newStatus`, `prevStatus` (with `taskId`), `task`, `projectData`, `isUpdateTask` for a status change; `firebaseObj` (`Task_Priority`), `taskData`, `priorityObj` (with `taskId`), `projectData` for a priority change; `firebaseObj` (`TaskName`), `taskData`, `obj`, `projectData` for a rename. The acting user is taken from the session, not the body. Answers with `data`: the result of the action, and statusText 'Task updated successfully.'. A task the caller cannot open answers 404 and a missing task permission answers 403; other failures answer `status` false with the error text. |
| `POST` | `/api/v3/timeTracker/start` | Session or token | undocumented | Starts a tracker timer on a task, for the desktop tracker. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. The caller can only start a timer for themselves; a `userId` naming someone else answers 403. Only users marked as tracker users may start one, else it answers `isPermissionDenied`. Reads `description`, `projectId` and `taskId` from the body, all required, plus `considerActionTime` (boolean) and `actionTime` (a Unix time in seconds) to set the start time. A start in an approved, locked period is refused. Answers with `statusText`: the id of the new time entry. |
| `POST` | `/api/v3/timetracker/capture` | Session or token | undocumented | Stores a tracker screenshot on a running timer and moves its end time forward. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `file`, `path`, `companyId`, `timeSheetId`, `key`, `imageName`, `prevscreenShot`, `screenShotTime`, `memoName` and `strokes` (a JSON string) from a form upload; all are required. `companyId` must match the company header, `timeSheetId` must be a timer of the caller that is still running, and `path` must lie in that timer's own capture folder, or it answers 403 (a path already stored answers 409). The image is the uploaded file. Answers with `statusText` `Data Update Succesfully`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v4/timetracker/capture` | Session or token | undocumented | Stores a tracker screenshot on a running timer and moves its end time forward, for the desktop tracker. Any signed-in member of the workspace may call it. Reads `file`, `path`, `companyId`, `timeSheetId`, `key`, `imageName`, `prevscreenShot`, `screenShotTime`, `memoName` and `strokes` (a JSON string) from a form upload; all are required. `companyId` must match the company header, `timeSheetId` must be a timer of the caller that is still running, and `path` must lie in that timer's own capture folder, or it answers 403 (a path already stored answers 409). The image is the uploaded file. Only tracker users may use it, else it answers `isPermissionDenied`. It also reads `considerActionTime` and `actionTime` (seconds) to set the end time. Answers with `statusText` `Data Update Succesfully`. |
